In a user study, exposing participants to goal-level analyses shifts their perceived contributions by nearly 2 points on a 5-point scale, revealing systematic miscalibration in how users understand their own AI-assisted work.

</details>


### [181] [Findings of the Fifth Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution: Expanding Datasets for Long-Range Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21369)
*Michal Novák,Miloslav Konopík,Anna Nedoluzhko,Martin Popel,Ondřej Pražák,Jakub Sido,Milan Straka,Zdeněk Žabokrtský,Daniel Zeman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了第五届多语言指代消解共享任务（CODI-CRAC 2026），重点强调长距离实体指代，扩展了5个新数据集和2种语言（基于CorefUD 1.4版本，涵盖19种语言27个数据集），共有10个系统参与（包括4个LLM方法），传统方法仍领先但LLM展现出巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动多语言指代消解技术的发展，特别是针对长距离实体的指代消解这一难点，通过扩展语言覆盖范围和引入新数据集来评估现有方法的泛化能力。

Method: 组织共享任务竞赛，参与者需开发能够进行指称识别和基于身份的核心指代聚类的系统。任务基于CorefUD 1.4多语言语料库，新增5个数据集和2种语言，重点关注跨越多个词和句子的长距离指代链。

Result: 共有10个系统参赛，其中4个为LLM方法（3个微调模型、1个少样本方法）。传统系统仍保持领先地位，但LLM方法展现出显著潜力。

Conclusion: 当前传统方法在多语言指代消解上仍占优势，但LLM方法展示了强劲的发展势头，有望在未来的版本中挑战甚至超越传统方法。

Abstract: This paper describes the fifth edition of the Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution, held in conjunction with the CODI-CRAC 2026 workshop. Building on previous iterations, the task required participants to develop systems capable of mention identification and identity-based coreference clustering.
  The 2026 edition specifically emphasizes long-range entities, defined as coreferential chains spanning significant distances, across many words and sentences.
  The task expanded its linguistic scope by incorporating five new datasets and two additional languages. These additions leverage version 1.4 of CorefUD, a harmonized multilingual collection comprising 27 datasets in 19 languages.
  In total, ten systems participated, including four LLM-based approaches (three fine-tuned models and one few-shot approach). While traditional systems still maintained their lead, LLMs demonstrated significant potential, suggesting they may soon challenge established approaches in future editions.

</details>


### [182] [Post-Hoc Understanding of Metaphor Processing in Decoder-Only Language Models via Conditional Scale Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21391)
*Lawhori Chakrabarti,Jennifer Johnson-Leung,Bert Baumgaertner,Aleksandar Vakanski,Min Xian,Boyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出条件尺度熵(CSE)这一基于小波的度量方法，用于分析transformer模型在处理隐喻时跨层的频率尺度参与模式。实验表明，在多个解码器架构(GPT-2系列、LLaMA-2 7B、GPT-oss 20B)中，隐喻性token在连续层位置表现出显著高于字面性token的谱宽度，该效应在早期到中间深度出现，且不能被语义复杂性或命题内容匹配所解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解transformer模型在深度方向上如何组织对token的重新解释（即隐喻处理中上下文意义偏离字面意义的过程），这是一个机制可解释性领域的开放问题。

Method: 提出了条件尺度熵(CSE)作为度量方法，该度量基于小波变换，能衡量transformer在每一层位置跨频率尺度的计算参与广度。两个定理证明了CSE对更新幅度的不变性，从而将更新的结构模式与强度分离开来。

Result: 在多个解码器架构（124M到20B参数）上，隐喻性token在连续层位置产生的谱宽度显著高于字面性token。该效应在早期到中间深度范围一致出现，通过了聚类置换校正检验，并在200个自然主义VUA对的独立分析中得到验证。特异性控制实验表明该效应不能被语义复杂性或匹配的命题内容所解释。

Conclusion: 多尺度协调是多层解码器架构中隐喻语言处理的一致特征标记，CSE是表征transformer中跨深度结构的一种原理性工具。

Abstract: Metaphor requires a language model to resolve a token whose contextual meaning diverges from its basic literal sense. Understanding how transformer models organize this reinterpretation across depth remains an open problem in mechanistic interpretability. We introduce conditional scale entropy (CSE), a wavelet-derived measure of how broadly transformer computation engages across frequency scales at each layer position. Two theorems establish that CSE is invariant to update magnitude, isolating the structural pattern of updates from their intensity. Using CSE, we find that metaphorical tokens produce significantly higher spectral breadth than literal tokens at contiguous layer positions on every decoder-only architecture tested, from 124M to 20B parameters (GPT-2 family, LLaMA-2 7B, GPT-oss 20B). The effect survives cluster-based permutation correction, recurs in the early-to-mid relative depth range across models, and converges with an independent analysis of 200 naturalistic VUA pairs. Specificity controls further show that the effect is not explained by semantic complexity or by matched propositional content. These results identify multi-scale coordination as a consistent signature of metaphorical language processing in the decoder-only architectures examined, and establish CSE as a principled tool for characterizing cross-depth structure in transformers.

</details>


### [183] [Mem-$π$: Adaptive Memory through Learning When and What to Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21463)
*Xiaoqiang Wang,Chao Wang,Hadi Nekoei,Christopher Pal,Alexandre Lacoste,Spandana Gella,Bang Liu,Perouz Taslakian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mem-π提出了一种基于生成式记忆的LLM智能体框架，使用专用语言/视觉语言模型按需生成上下文相关的指导，替代传统检索式记忆，并在多项基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强智能体依赖相似性检索从情景记忆库或技能库中返回静态条目，这些条目常常与当前上下文不匹配，导致指导效果有限。

Method: 使用独立于下游智能体的专用语言/视觉语言模型，基于当前智能体上下文联合决策何时生成指导以及生成什么指导；采用决策-内容解耦的强化学习目标进行训练。

Result: 在网页导航、终端工具使用、基于文本的具身交互等多个智能体基准上，Mem-π一致优于检索式方法和先前基于强化学习优化的记忆基线，在网页导航任务上实现超过30%的相对改进。

Conclusion: 生成式记忆范式能够提供比检索式记忆更精准的上下文对齐指导，在多样化的智能体任务中展现出更强的泛化能力和性能优势。

Abstract: We present Mem-$π$, a framework for adaptive memory in large language model (LLM) agents, where useful guidance is generated on demand rather than retrieved from external memory stores. Existing memory-augmented agents typically rely on similarity-based retrieval from episodic memory banks or skill libraries, returning static entries that often misalign with the current context. In contrast, Mem-$π$ uses a dedicated language or vision-language model with its own parameters, separate from the downstream agent, to generate context-specific guidance for complex tasks. Conditioned on the current agent context, the model jointly decides when to produce guidance and what guidance to produce. We train it with a decision-content decoupled reinforcement learning (RL) objective, enabling it to abstain when generation would not help and otherwise produce concise, useful guidance. Across diverse agentic benchmarks spanning web navigation, terminal-based tool use, and text-based embodied interaction, Mem-$π$ consistently outperforms retrieval-based and prior RL-optimized memory baselines, achieving over 30% relative improvement on web navigation tasks.

</details>


### [184] [Leveraging LLMs for Grammar Adaptation: A Study on Metamodel-Grammar Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21465)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Rahul Sharma,Regina Hebig,Daniel Strüber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的方法，通过学习历史版本中的语法适配信息，自动为模型驱动工程中元模型演化后的语法进行适配，在复杂语法场景下优于传统规则方法（测试集达到100%适配一致性），但在大规模语法（297条规则）场景下表现不足（低于90%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型驱动工程中，元模型演化后需要相应地适配语法以保持一致性，而现有手工方式繁琐，规则方法在复杂语法场景下存在局限性。

Method: 基于LLM的方法，通过学习先前版本的语法适配信息，自动将适配应用到演化后的新语法上。使用4个DSL作为训练集开发提示策略，2个DSL作为测试集进行验证，并在QVTo上进行纵向案例研究。使用三种LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.5, ChatGPT 5.1, Gemini 3），从语法规则级适配一致性、输出相似度和元模型符合度三个维度评估。

Result: 测试集上三种LLM均实现100%适配一致性和输出相似度，而规则方法仅在DOT上达到84.21%、在Xcore上达到62.50%。QVTo纵向研究中LLM方法三步演化均无需手动编辑语法。但在EAST-ADL（297条规则）大规模语法上，LLM适配一致性远低于90%。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的方法在复杂语法场景下具有优势，但在大规模语法适配方面存在局限性。

Abstract: In model-driven engineering, metamodel evolution leads to the need to adapt corresponding grammars to maintain consistency, which typically requires tedious manual work. Existing rule-based methods can achieve partial automation but have limitations when handling complex grammar scenarios. This paper proposes a Large Language Model-based approach that automatically applies adaptations to new grammars after evolution by learning grammar adaptations from previous versions. We evaluated this approach on six real-world Xtext domain-specific languages, using four DSLs as a training set to develop prompting strategies, two DSLs as a test set for validation, and conducting a longitudinal case study on QVTo. The evaluation used three Large Language Models (Claude Sonnet 4.5, ChatGPT 5.1, Gemini 3) and measured grammar adaptation quality from three dimensions: grammar rule-level adaptation consistency, output similarity, and metamodel conformance. Results show that on the test set, all three LLMs achieved 100% adaptation consistency and output similarity, while the rule-based approach achieved only 84.21% on DOT and 62.50% on Xcore. In the QVTo longitudinal study, the LLM-based approach successfully reused learned adaptations across all three evolution steps without manual grammar editing, while the rule-based approach required manual adjustments in two of three transitions. However, on large-scale grammars (EAST-ADL, 297 rules), LLMs' adaptation consistency was far below 90%. This study demonstrates the advantages of LLM-based approaches in handling complex grammar scenarios, while revealing their limitations in large-scale grammar adaptation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [SOLAR: A Self-Optimizing Open-Ended Autonomous Agent for Lifelong Learning and Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20189)
*Nitin Vetcha,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SOLAR是一种自优化终身学习推理器，通过参数级元学习和多级强化学习，将模型权重作为探索环境，实现无需梯度更新的测试时自适应，有效应对概念漂移，在常识、数学、医学、编码、社交和逻辑推理任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在动态真实场景部署时面临概念漂移和高昂梯度更新成本两大瓶颈，传统微调难以适应非平稳数据流，容易灾难性遗忘或需要大量人工数据整理。

Method: SOLAR将模型权重视为探索环境，利用参数级元学习进行自改进；采用多级强化学习自主发现适应策略，实现测试时高效适应未见领域；维护一个演化中的有效修改策略知识库，作为情节记忆缓冲以平衡可塑性与稳定性。

Result: 在常识、数学、医学、编码、社交和逻辑推理任务上，SOLAR均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: SOLAR标志着向能够在演化环境中进行终身自适应的自主智能体迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of large language models (LLMs), they still face bottlenecks while deploying in dynamic, real-world settings with primary challenges being concept drift and the high cost of gradient-based adaptation. Traditional fine-tuning (FT) struggles to adapt to non-stationary data streams without resulting in catastrophic for getting or requiring extensive manual data curation. To address these limitations within the streaming and continual learning paradigm, we propose the Self-Optimizing Lifelong Autonomous Reasoner (SOLAR) which is an open-ended autonomous agent that leverages parameter-level meta-learning to self-improve, treating model weights as an environment for exploration. It initiates the process by consolidating a strong prior over common-sense knowledge making it effective for transfer-learning. By utilizing a multi-level reinforcement learning approach, SOLAR autonomously discovers adaptation strategies, enabling efficient test-time adaptation to unseen domains. Crucially, SOLAR maintains an evolving knowledge base of valid modification strategies, implicitly acting as an episodic memory buffer to balance plasticity (adaptation to new tasks) and stability (retention of meta-knowledge). Experiments demonstrate that SOLAR outperforms strong baselines on common-sense, mathematical, medical, coding, social and logical reasoning tasks, marking a significant step toward autonomous agents capable of lifelong adaptation in evolving environments.

</details>


### [186] [AgentCo-op: Retrieval-Based Synthesis of Interoperable Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20425)
*Shuaike Shen,Wenduo Cheng,Shike Wang,Mingqian Ma,Jian Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentCo-op是一个基于检索的合成框架，通过类型化工件传递组合可重用的技能、工具和外部智能体成为可执行工作流，并在执行失败时对有问题的组件进行有界的自引导局部修复。在两个开放世界基因组学案例研究和六个编码、数学及问答基准测试中，AgentCo-op实现了四个基准的最佳结果和统一骨干设置下的最佳平均分数，同时降低了每任务成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放式的科学环境中，多智能体工作流设计面临三大困难：缺乏精心策划的训练集、可靠的标量评估指标，以及现有工具与智能体之间缺乏标准化接口。现有方法多针对有基准优化的场景，难以推广到开放世界。

Method: AgentCo-op采用检索式合成框架：(1) 通过类型化工件（typed artifacts）传递组合可重用的技能、工具和外部智能体为可执行工作流；(2) 当执行证据表明失败时，对有问题的组件进行有界自引导局部修复（bounded self-guided local repair）；(3) 可将搜索到的工作流作为结构先验导入，通过检索到的组件对节点进行落地并应用局部修复来改进工作流。

Result: 在两个开放世界基因组学案例（空间转录组学和基因集解释的协作发现、单细胞多组学数据的跨模态标记分析）中成功组成可审计工作流。在六个编码/数学/问答基准上，AgentCo-op在四个基准上取得最佳结果，在统一骨干设置下取得最佳平均分数，并持续降低每任务成本。

Conclusion: 检索式合成可以将自动化智能体工作流设计从基准优化的智能体图扩展到由现有智能体、工具和类型化工件构建的开放世界工作流，并且合成与搜索具有互补性。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent workflows is especially difficult in open-ended scientific settings where tasks lack curated training sets, reliable scalar evaluation metrics, and standardized interfaces between existing tools and agents. We propose AgentCo-op, a retrieval-based synthesis framework that composes reusable skills, tools, and external agents into executable workflows through typed artifact handoffs, then applies bounded self-guided local repair to implicated components when execution evidence indicates failure. In two open-world genomics case studies, AgentCo-op composes independently developed scientific agents and external tool repositories into auditable workflows without redesigning them or running global topology search. It coordinates specialized agents for spatial transcriptomics and gene-set interpretation to enable collaborative discovery from spatial transcriptomics data, and builds a parallel workflow for cross-modality marker analysis on single-cell multiome data. AgentCo-op can also import a searched workflow as a structural prior and improve it by grounding nodes with retrieved components and applying local repair, showing that synthesis and search are complementary. On six coding, math, and question-answering benchmarks, AgentCo-op achieves the best result on four benchmarks and the best average score under a unified backbone setting, while consistently reducing per-task cost relative to multi-agent baselines. Together, these results suggest that retrieval-based synthesis can extend automated agentic workflow design beyond benchmark-optimized agent graphs to open-world workflows built from existing agents, tools, and typed artifacts.

</details>


### [187] [\ECUAS{n}: A family of metrics for principled evaluation of uncertainty-augmented systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20490)
*Lautaro Estienne,Erik Ernst,Matías Vera,Pablo Piantanida,Luciana Ferrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有不确定性增强（UA）系统的评估方法（如使用分离指标、固定拒绝成本或覆盖-风险曲线）存在不足，并提出了一种新的指标族ECUAS(n)，将其形式化为任务相关的适当评分规则，参数n控制预测错误成本与不确定性不完美的权衡，在理论和实验上证明了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险自动决策场景中，预测不确定性信息对用户接受或拒绝预测至关重要。然而，当前文献中对同时输出预测和不确定性分数的UA系统的评估方式（分离指标、固定拒绝成本、覆盖-风险曲线积分等）不足以评估其在不确定性下的整体决策表现，亟需更合适的评估指标。

Method: 提出ECUAS(n)指标族，将其构造为任务相关的适当评分规则（proper scoring rules），通过参数n调节预测错误成本与不确定性不完美之间的权衡。

Result: 在多种分类和生成数据集（包括TriviaQA人工标注子集）上的理论和实验表明，ECUAS(n)指标相比现有评估方法更能有效评估UA系统的整体决策表现。

Conclusion: ECUAS(n)为不确定性增强系统的决策性能提供了更合理、统一的评估框架，灵活适应不同应用场景的成本权衡需求。

Abstract: In high-stakes automated decision-making, access to predictive uncertainty is essential for enabling users -- human or downstream systems -- to accept or reject predictions based on application-specific cost trade-offs. Such uncertainty-augmented (UA) systems -- i.e., systems that output both predictions and uncertainty scores -- are currently being assessed in the literature in a variety of ways, using separate metrics to evaluate the predictions and the uncertainty scores, setting a cost function with a fixed rejection cost or integrating over a coverage-risk curve. We argue that these evaluation approaches are inadequate for assessing overall performance of the UA system for decision making under uncertainty and propose a novel family of metrics, \ECUAS{n}, formulated as proper scoring rules for the task of interest. The parameter $n$ controls the trade-off between the cost of incorrect predictions and imperfect uncertainties depending on the needs of the use-case. We demonstrate the advantages of the \ECUAS{n} metrics both theoretically and empirically, through experiments on diverse classification and generation datasets, including a manually annotated subset of TriviaQA.

</details>


### [188] [Open-World Evaluations for Measuring Frontier AI Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20520)
*Sayash Kapoor,Peter Kirgis,Andrew Schwartz,Stephan Rabanser,J. J. Allaire,Rishi Bommasani,Harry Coppock,Magda Dubois,Gillian K Hadfield,Andrew B. Hall,Sara Hooker,Seth Lazar,Steve Newman,Dimitris Papailiopoulos,Shoshannah Tekofsky,Helen Toner,Cozmin Ududec,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有基准评估存在高估或低估能力的局限，提出开放式评估（open-world evaluations）作为补充：在长期、混乱的真实世界任务中通过小样本定性分析进行评估。介绍了CRUX项目，通过让AI智能体独立开发并发布iOS应用到App Store的实例，验证了该评估方法能提前预警即将普及的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基准的评估虽然重要，但存在显著局限：它偏向于可精确定义、自动评分、易于优化、低预算和短周期的任务，因此既可能高估也可能低估AI在部署环境中的真实能力。需要开发互补性评估方法来更准确追踪前沿AI进展。

Method: 提出开放式评估范式：采用长期、开放、真实世界任务，通过小样本定性分析而非大规模自动化评分进行评估。具体通过CRUX项目实践，设计一个AI智能体从零开发并发布一个简单iOS应用到Apple App Store的完整任务，观察其完成过程中的自主性和所需人工干预次数。

Result: AI智能体在完成iOS应用开发到发布的全流程中，仅出现一次可避免的人工干预，成功完成任务。

Conclusion: 开放式评估能够提供对AI能力更真实、更具预警性的评估，揭示出基准测试可能遗漏的能力信号。建议将开放式评估作为基准评估的补充方法，并在设计和报告开放式评估时遵循特定规范。

Abstract: Benchmark-based evaluation remains important for tracking frontier AI progress. But it can both overstate and understate deployed capability because it privileges tasks that can be precisely specified, automatically graded, easy to optimize for, and run with low budgets and short time horizons. We advocate for a complementary class of evaluations, which we term open-world evaluations: long-horizon, messy, real-world tasks assessed through small-sample qualitative analysis rather than benchmark-scale automation. In this paper we survey recent open-world evaluations, identify their strengths and limitations, and introduce CRUX (Collaborative Research for Updating AI eXpectations), a project for conducting such evaluations regularly. As a first instance, we task an AI agent with developing and publishing a simple iOS application to the Apple App Store. The agent completed the task with only a single avoidable manual intervention, suggesting that open-world evaluations can provide early warning of capabilities that may soon become widespread. We conclude with recommendations for designing and reporting open-world evals.

</details>


### [189] [AgentAtlas: Beyond Outcome Leaderboards for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20530)
*Parsa Mazaheri,Kasra Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentAtlas提出了一种针对大语言模型智能体评估的细粒度方法论框架，包含六状态控制-决策分类法、九类轨迹失败分类法、分类法感知与盲测对比方法以及基准覆盖审计，实验发现移除标签菜单后所有模型轨迹准确率下降14-40个百分点，且无单一模型在所有维度上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体评估基准碎片化严重，各自采用不同的度量单位（任务成功率、工具调用正确性、一致性、安全性、鲁棒性），单一准确率指标已不足以评估可部署智能体的实际能力。

Method: 提出四个核心组件：(1) 六状态控制-决策分类法（执行/询问/拒绝/停止/确认/恢复）；(2) 九类轨迹失败分类法及两个正交层次化标签（主要错误来源、影响）；(3) 分类法感知与盲测对比方法，量化提示中监督信息对模型能力的贡献；(4) 基准覆盖审计，将15个智能体基准映射到六个行为轴。在8个模型（4个闭源前沿、4个开源）共1,342个生成项上进行实验。

Result: 移除显式标签菜单后，所有模型的轨迹准确率下降14-40个百分点，收敛到0.54-0.62的狭窄区间，且无单一模型能在控制准确率、轨迹诊断及工具上下文效用保持三个维度上同时胜出。

Conclusion: 现有基准评估结果可能高估了模型的实际能力，部分能力源自提示中的监督信息；需要一个更细粒度、多维度的评估框架来全面衡量可部署智能体的真实性能。

Abstract: Large language model agents now act on codebases, browsers, operating systems, calendars, files, and tool ecosystems, but the benchmarks used to evaluate them are fragmented: each emphasizes a different unit of measurement (final task success, tool-call validity, repeated-pass consistency, trajectory safety, or attack robustness). A line of 2024-2025 work has converged on the diagnosis that a single accuracy column is no longer the right unit of comparison for deployable agents. AgentAtlas extends this line of work with four components: (i) a six-state control-decision taxonomy (Act / Ask / Refuse / Stop / Confirm / Recover); (ii) a nine-category trajectory-failure taxonomy with two orthogonal hierarchical labels (primary_error_source, impact); (iii) a taxonomy-aware vs. taxonomy-blind methodology that measures how much of a model's apparent capability comes from the supervision in the prompt; and (iv) a benchmark-coverage audit mapping fifteen agent benchmarks against six behavioral axes. To demonstrate the methodology we run a small fixed eight-model set (1,342 generated items, four frontier closed and four open-weight) under both prompt modes. Removing the explicit label menu drops every model's trajectory accuracy by 14-40 pp to a tight 0.54-0.62 floor regardless of family, and no single model wins on all three of control accuracy, trajectory diagnosis, and tool-context utility retention. We treat the synthetic run as a measurement-protocol demonstration, not a benchmark release.

</details>


### [190] [Mahjax: A GPU-Accelerated Mahjong Simulator for Reinforcement Learning in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20577)
*Soichiro Nishimori,Shinri Okano,Keigo Habara,Sotetsu Koyamada,Eason Yu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mahjax，一个基于JAX的全向量化日本麻将环境，支持GPU大规模并行化，吞吐量达每秒200万步（无赤牌规则）和100万步（有赤牌规则），并验证了其在强化学习训练中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本麻将具有多玩家、不完美信息、随机性和高维状态空间等特性，这些挑战与强化学习中的复杂现实决策问题相似。现有研究多依赖人类对局日志进行监督预训练，而具备从零学习能力的算法（如AlphaZero系列）具有更广泛的适用性，因此需要高效的环境支持。

Method: 在JAX中实现完全向量化的日本麻将环境Mahjax，利用GPU进行大规模并行化推演，并提供高质量可视化工具用于调试和交互。

Result: 在八块NVIDIA A100 GPU上，无赤牌规则下达到每秒200万步，有赤牌规则下达到每秒100万步；同时验证了在该环境下训练的智能体能够有效提升对基线策略的排名表现。

Conclusion: Mahjax为日本麻将的从零强化学习研究提供了高效、可扩展的环境支持，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Riichi Mahjong is a multi-player, imperfect-information game characterized by stochasticity and high-dimensional state spaces. These attributes present a unique combination of challenges that mirror complex real-world decision-making problems in reinforcement learning. While prior research has heavily relied on supervised learning from human play logs to pre-train the policy, algorithms capable of learning \textit{tabula rasa} (from scratch) offer greater potential for general applicability, as evidenced by the AlphaZero lineage. To facilitate such research, we introduce \textbf{Mahjax}, a fully vectorized Riichi Mahjong environment implemented in JAX to enable large-scale rollout parallelization on Graphics Processing Units (GPUs). We also provide a high-quality visualization tool to streamline debugging and interaction with trained agents. Experimental results demonstrate that Mahjax achieves throughputs of up to \textbf{2 million} and \textbf{1 million steps per second} on eight NVIDIA A100 GPUs under the no-red and red rules, respectively. Furthermore, we validate the environment's utility for reinforcement learning by showing that agents can be trained effectively to improve their rank against baseline policies.

</details>


### [191] [COAgents: Multi-Agent Framework to Learn and Navigate Routing Problems Search Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20618)
*Oleksandr Yakovenko,Mahdi Mostajabdaveh,Cheikh Ahmed,Abdullah Ali Sivas,Xiaorui Li,Zirui Zhou,Mao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COAgents是一个用于车辆路径问题（VRP）的协作式多智能体框架，将搜索过程建模为图结构，通过节点选择、移动选择和跳跃三个智能体分别负责局部精化、局部改进和全局探索，在CVRP和VRPTW基准上取得了竞争性或最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆路径问题（VRP）在大规模场景下因组合复杂性而计算困难，传统启发式方法依赖手工规则进行局部改进和跳出局部最优，但难以在不同实例间泛化。

Method: 提出COAgents框架，将搜索过程建模为图（节点为解，边为局部改进或大扰动跳跃）；动态构建部分搜索图（PSG）；训练节点选择智能体、移动选择智能体（指导精化）和跳跃智能体（触发探索）；问题无关的搜索控制与领域特定编码分离。

Result: 在CVRP和VRPTW基准上，COAgents在CVRP上与多个学习搜索基线相当；在VRPTW上，相比最强神经求解器POMO，在N=100时缩小与最优解差距14%，N=50时缩小44%；相比ALNS分别缩小21%和40%。

Conclusion: COAgents通过多智能体协作和搜索图建模，有效平衡了局部精化与全局探索，在更具挑战性的VRPTW实例上达到了学习型方法的新最优水平，且框架设计具有良好的任务适应性。

Abstract: Although Vehicle Routing Problems (VRP) are essential to many real-world systems, they remain computationally intractable at scale due to their combinatorial complexity. Traditional heuristics rely on handcrafted rules for local improvements and occasional \textit{jumps} to escape local minima, but often struggle to generalize across diverse instances. We introduce \textbf{COAgents}, a cooperative multi-agent framework that models the search process as a graph: nodes represent solutions, and edges correspond to either local refinements or large perturbations for diversification (i.e., jumps). A \textit{Partial Search Graph} (PSG) is dynamically constructed during search, enabling COAgents to train a Node Selection Agent and a Move Selection Agent to guide intensification, and a Jump Agent to trigger well-timed explorations of new regions. Unlike end-to-end learning approaches, COAgents cleanly separates problem-agnostic search control from compact domain-specific encoding, facilitating adaptability across tasks. Extensive experiments on the CVRP and VRPTW benchmarks show that COAgents remains competitive with several learn-to-search baselines on CVRP and sets a new state of the art among learning-based methods on the more challenging VRPTW instances, reducing the gap to the best-known solutions by 14\% at $N\!=\!100$ and 44\% at $N\!=\!50$ relative to the strongest neural solver (POMO), and by 21\% and 40\% respectively relative to ALNS.
  Code is available at https://github.com/mahdims/COAgents.

</details>


### [192] [Evaluating Temporal Semantic Caching and Workflow Optimization in Agentic Plan-Execute Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20630)
*Alimurtaza Mustafa Merchant,Krish Veera,Sajal Kumar Goyla,Shambhawi Bhure,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对工业资产运维工作流中LLM管道延迟高的问题，提出时间语义缓存和MCP工作流优化两层加速方案，在AssetOpsBench上分别实现30.6倍（缓存命中）和1.67倍加速，并揭示纯语义缓存在参数密集型工业查询中的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业资产运维工作流对延迟高度敏感，单次用户查询需协调传感器数据、工单、故障模式、预测工具和领域智能体。现有KV缓存重用和基于嵌入的语义缓存专为聊天服务设计，当输出有效性依赖时间、资产或传感器参数时失效，因此需要针对工业场景的专用缓存与优化方案。

Method: 提出两层优化：(1) 时间语义缓存（Temporal Semantic Cache），考虑时间维度上的语义相似性；(2) MCP工作流优化，包括磁盘支持的tool-discovery缓存和依赖感知的并行步骤执行。

Result: MCP工作流优化实现1.67倍加速，中位数端到端延迟降低约40.0%；时间语义缓存命中时实现中位数30.6倍加速。同时揭示了纯语义缓存在参数丰富的工业查询中的具体失败模式。

Conclusion: 提出的两层优化方案在工业资产运维场景下显著降低了LLM管道延迟，并提供了关于缓存选择如何影响MCP基准评估正确性的关键分析。

Abstract: Industrial asset operations workflows are latency-sensitive because a single user query may require coordination over sensor data, work orders, failure modes, forecasting tools, and domain-specific agents. We evaluate this problem on AssetOpsBench (AOB), an industrial agent benchmark whose plan-execute pipeline exposes repeated overhead from tool discovery, LLM planning, MCP tool execution, and final summarization. Existing LLM caching techniques such as KV-cache reuse and embedding-based semantic caching were designed for chatbot serving and break down when output validity depends on time, asset, or sensor parameters. We propose two complementary optimization layers for AOB plan-execute pipelines: a temporal semantic cache and a set of MCP workflow optimizations combining disk-backed tool-discovery caching and dependency-aware parallel step execution. MCP workflow optimizations corresponded to a 1.67x speedup and reduced median end-to-end latency by about 40.0% while the temporal-cache benchmark achieved a median of 30.6x speedup on cache hits. Beyond the speedup, our results expose a concrete failure mode of pure semantic caching for parameter-rich industrial queries, providing a critical analysis of how caching choices interact with evaluation correctness in MCP-backed agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [193] [Declarative Data Services: Structured Agentic Discovery for Composing Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20690)
*Shanshan Ye,Duo Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出声明式数据服务（DDS）架构，通过四层类型化契约将异构数据系统的组合搜索分解为有界子搜索，解决了无界智能体发现在数据系统组合中无法收敛的问题，在交易后端工作负载上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无界智能体发现在异构数据系统后端搜索时面临搜索空间异构、验证器依赖实际部署运行、组合知识在预训练中不均匀捕获三大难题，即使增加迭代和显式组合知识也无法稳定收敛到可工作的系统堆栈。

Method: DDS架构定义了四层类型化契约：意图层、算子DAG层、每系统技能层、运行时归因层，将全局搜索分解为有界子搜索；子智能体搜索各自的类型化空间，知识通过内联技能引用前向传递，错误通过类型化信号反向传播。

Result: 在交易后端工作负载上，DDS在无界发现无法收敛的场景下成功收敛；运行时失败转化为技能补丁，被后续部署内联引用。

Conclusion: DDS作为早期原型，为真实世界数据系统组合提供了可收敛的结构化智能体发现范式，验证了类型化契约分解搜索空间的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic discovery has shown that LLM-driven search can find novel algorithms, designs, and code under benchmark conditions. Translating the paradigm to multi-system data backends surfaces a harder problem: the search space is heterogeneous, the verifier is whether a deployed stack actually runs, and composition knowledge is unevenly captured in pretraining. Unbounded agentic discovery, a coding agent iterating on failure-log feedback, fails to converge consistently on a working stack even when iteration and explicit composition knowledge are added. We propose Declarative Data Services (DDS), an architecture for structured agentic discovery of data-system compositions from declarative user intent. The framework owns four typed contracts at successive layers (intent, operator DAG, per-system skills, runtime attribution) that decompose the global search into bounded sub-searches; sub-agents search each typed space, while the framework provides the channels by which knowledge flows forward as inline skill citations and errors route backward as typed signals. As a proof of life on a trading-backend workload, DDS converges where unbounded discovery does not; runtime failures become skill patches that the next deployment cites inline. We position this as an early prototype reporting lessons from real-world data-system composition.

</details>


### [194] [VBFDD-Agent for Electric Vehicle Battery Fault Detection and Diagnosis: Descriptive Text Modeling of Battery Digital Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20742)
*Joey Chan,Zhen Chen,Ershun Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VBFDD-Agent，一种基于大语言模型和结构化描述文本的车辆电池故障检测与诊断智能体，将电池信号转化为自然语言语料库，结合历史案例检索和维修手册，实现可解释的故障诊断与维护建议生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电池故障诊断方法针对特定场景设计，跨域适应性差，缺乏统一的知识表示；同时开源电池故障报告语料稀缺，限制了人机协作和智能化诊断的发展。

Method: 1) 将电池监测信号、统计特征、异常记录和状态评估结果转化为结构化自然语言描述，构建电池健康诊断语料库；2) 提出VBFDD-Agent框架，整合描述性电池状态文本、历史案例检索、本地维修手册和大语言模型推理，生成结构化诊断结果与维护建议。

Result: 实验表明该方法能基于描述性文本表示准确进行异常监测，生成灵活、高效、可操作的维护建议；专家评估验证了所生成建议的实用价值。

Conclusion: VBFDD-Agent将传统电池诊断从标签预测扩展为可解释、面向维护的决策支持，提升了跨域适应性和人机协作能力。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of electric vehicles, the safety and reliability of lithium-ion batteries have become critical concerns. Effective anomaly detection is essential for ensuring safe battery operation. However, as battery systems and operating scenarios become increasingly complex, battery fault diagnosis and maintenance require stronger cross-domain adaptability and human-AI collaboration. Traditional fault detection and diagnosis methods are usually designed for specific scenarios and predefined workflows, making them less effective in complex real-world applications.
  To address the scarcity of open-source battery fault report corpora and the lack of unified maintenance knowledge representation, this study proposes a descriptive text modeling approach for battery signal reports. Monitoring signals, statistical features, anomaly records, and state assessment results are transformed into structured and readable natural language descriptions, forming a language corpus for battery health diagnosis and maintenance.
  Based on this corpus, we propose VBFDD-Agent, a vehicle battery fault detection and diagnosis agent for automotive-grade battery systems. VBFDD-Agent integrates descriptive battery-state texts, historical case retrieval, local maintenance manuals, and large language model reasoning to generate structured diagnostic results and maintenance recommendations. Experiments show that the proposed framework can accurately perform anomaly monitoring based on descriptive textual representations and provide flexible, efficient, and actionable maintenance suggestions. Expert evaluation further confirms the practical value of the generated recommendations. Overall, VBFDD-Agent extends traditional battery diagnosis from label prediction to interpretable and maintenance-oriented decision support.

</details>


### [195] [Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance for Flow Models under Compositional Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20758)
*Xuehui Yu,Fucheng Cai,Meiyi Wang,Xiaopeng Fan,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance (g^car)，一种轻量级可学习方法，通过动态检测和解决梯度冲突来纠正推理时引导采样中的偏离数据流形漂移问题，在多个领域（合成数据集、图像编辑、生成式决策规划）中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导采样方法在组合多个约束时常常失败，导致生成结果偏离真实数据流形（off-manifold drift），且近似误差随梯度不对齐而严重放大。

Method: 提出 Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance (g^car)，一种轻量级可学习方法，通过动态检测和解决梯度冲突来主动纠正偏离流形漂移。

Result: 在合成数据集、图像编辑、生成式决策规划等多个领域验证，g^car有效纠正偏离流形漂移，在生成保真度上超越基线方法，且计算开销轻量。

Conclusion: g^car能够在不进行微调的情况下有效处理多约束引导生成中的梯度冲突问题，提升生成质量，具有跨领域的通用性和高效性。

Abstract: Inference-time guided sampling steers state-of-the-art diffusion and flow models without fine-tuning by interpreting the generation process as a controllable trajectory. This provides a simple and flexible way to inject external constraints (e.g., cost functions or pre-trained verifiers) for controlled generation. However, existing methods often fail when composing multiple constraints simultaneously, which leads to deviations from the true data manifold. In this work, we identify root causes of this off-manifold drift and find that the approximation error scales severely with gradient misalignment. Building on these findings, we propose Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance ($g^\text{car}$), a lightweight and learnable method, which actively rectifies off-manifold drift by dynamically detecting and resolving gradient conflicts. We validate $g^\text{car}$ across diverse domains, ranging from synthetic datasets and image editing to generative decision-making for planning and control. Our results demonstrate that $g^\text{car}$ effectively rectifies off-manifold drift, surpassing baselines in generation fidelity while using light compute. Code is available at https://github.com/yuxuehui/CAR-guidance.

</details>


### [196] [Interaction Locality in Hierarchical Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20784)
*Yosuke Miyanishi,Tetsuro Morimura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出交互局部性（Interaction Locality）框架，用于衡量空间推理中信息流是否在邻近格子或语义片段内流动，或跨越它们。通过激活修补等方法分析HRM、TRM和MTU3D等模型，发现递归模型在局部写入、累积构建全局结构，而3D场景模型的空间因果性集中在模块边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理需要同时处理局部（位置相关）计算和全局（位置无关）结构，但缺乏统一框架来量化信息流是在局部区域内还是跨区域流动，因此需要任务几何感知的度量方法。

Method: 提出交互局部性框架，采用稀疏自编码器特征消融和有限噪声激活修补（附录包含结构雅可比和注意力检查），在HRM、TRM两个层次递归推理模型及MTU3D大尺度3D场景定位模型上应用分析。

Result: 激活修补揭示：递归模型中高层循环状态在邻近格子或同段单元内写入信息，重复递归更新将局部写入累积为全局解结构；该模式在迷宫、数独、ARC-AGI上一致，TRM中最为集中。MTU3D中因果空间局部性主要出现在视觉特征向下游模块传递的边界，而非视觉编码器内部均匀分布。

Conclusion: 交互局部性将直觉上的"局部执行/全局规划"转化为可复现的测量框架，且递归模型与3D模型在空间因果结构分布上存在本质差异——前者依赖递归动力学实现局部到全局的接力，后者集中在模块边界。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires both location-bound computation and location-invariant structure: agents must make local moves while preserving route, object, or constraint-level plans. We propose interaction locality, a task-geometry-aware framework for measuring whether information flow stays within nearby cells or semantic segments, or crosses them. We instantiate the framework with sparse-autoencoder feature ablations and finite-noise activation patching, with structural Jacobian and attention checks reported in the appendix, and apply it to HRM and TRM, two compact hierarchical and recursive reasoning models, on Maze-Hard, Sudoku Extreme, and ARC-AGI. Across these models, activation patching gives the clearest architectural fingerprint: high-level recurrent states tend to write information within nearby cells or same-segment units, while repeated recursive updates accumulate these local writes into broader solution structure. This pattern holds across maze paths, Sudoku constraints, and ARC-AGI object neighborhoods, with the strongest concentration in TRM. To test whether interaction locality extends beyond toy-yet-challenging grid benchmarks, we also apply it to MTU3D, a large-scale embodied 3D scene-grounding model. In this MTU3D setting, causal spatial locality appears primarily at the transition where visual scene features are handed to the downstream grounding module, rather than uniformly throughout the visual encoder. This contrast suggests that the local-to-global handoff observed in HRM and TRM is tied to explicit recursive reasoning dynamics, while embodied 3D models may concentrate causal spatial structure at module boundaries. Interaction locality turns the intuitive local-execution/global-planning story into a reproducible measurement framework for recursive and embodied spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [197] [Conditional Equivalence of DPO and RLHF: Implicit Assumption, Failure Modes, and Provable Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20834)
*Zhiqin Yang,Yonggang Zhang,Wei Xue,Dong Fang,Bo Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明DPO与RLHF的理论等价性是有条件的，当关键假设被违反时两者优化目标不同。提出带约束的偏好优化方法CPO，实现可证明的对齐，并在标准基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示DPO与RLHF理论等价性的条件性局限——当RLHF最优策略偏好人类偏好响应这一隐含假设被违反时，DPO会优化与人类偏好对齐不同的目标，导致病态收敛。

Method: 1) 理论分析证明DPO和RLHF在假设违反时优化不同目标；2) 引入约束偏好优化(CPO)，在RLHF基础上增加约束以实现可证明对齐；3) 通过软间隔排序进行几何解释，揭示DPO可能使用负目标实现间隔排序。

Result: CPO在标准基准测试上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 建立了DPO保证成立的条件，并提供了在保持简洁性的同时实现可证明对齐的解决方案。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a popular alternative to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), offering theoretical equivalence with simpler implementation. We prove this equivalence is conditional rather than universal, depending on an implicit assumption frequently violated in practice: the RLHF-optimal policy must prefer human-preferred responses. When this assumption fails, DPO optimizes relative advantage over the reference policy rather than absolute alignment with human preferences, leading to pathological convergence where policies decrease DPO loss while preferring dispreferred responses. We characterize when this assumption is violated, show the existence of an undesirable solution space, and prove that DPO and RLHF optimize fundamentally different objectives in such cases. To address this, we introduce Constrained Preference Optimization (CPO), augmenting RLHF with constraints for provable alignment. We further provide a geometric interpretation through soft margin ranking, revealing that DPO implements margin ranking with potentially negative targets. Our theoretical analysis establishes when DPOs' guarantees hold and provides solutions preserving simplicity with provable alignment. Comprehensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that CPO achieves state-of-the-art performance. Code is available at: https://github.com/visitworld123/CPO.

</details>


### [198] [Governance by Construction for Generalist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20874)
*Segev Shlomov,Iftach Shoham,Alon Oved,Ido Levy,Sami Marreed,Harold Ship,Offer Akrabi,Sergey Zeltyn,Avi Yaeli,Nir Mashkif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CUGA提出了一种模块化策略即代码（policy-as-code）治理系统，通过五个结构化检查点（意图守卫、剧本、工具指南、人工审批、输出格式化）对通用LLM智能体进行运行时治理，无需微调即可实现可预测、可审计、符合合规要求的复合工作流执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级智能体需要在无领域重建的前提下，明确指定允许的操作、人工监督时机及信息暴露范围，现有方法缺乏内建治理机制。

Method: 提出CUGA策略系统，采用模块化策略即代码层与通用LLM智能体组合，在五个关键执行阶段（规划前、系统提示、工具调用边界、推理循环外、输出阶段）嵌入策略拦截点，实现持续治理。

Result: 在医疗场景中展示了动态剧本注入执行工具序列强制、意图守卫阻止恶意/意外有害请求、高风险操作的人工审批检查点，提升了策略遵守度和执行一致性。

Conclusion: 类型化治理原语能够加速企业智能体系统的安全部署，在不进行模型微调的情况下实现可预测、可审计、合规感知的行为。

Abstract: Enterprise agents are increasingly expected to operate autonomously across tools and interfaces, yet production deployments require governance by construction. Systems must specify which actions are allowed, when human oversight is required, and what information may be exposed, without rebuilding the agent for each domain. This demo presents CUGA's policy system, a modular policy-as-code layer that composes with a generalist LLM agent to deliver predictable, auditable, and compliance-aware behavior in compound workflows without model fine-tuning. We present a runtime governance architecture that enforces policy interventions at every critical stage of execution. Rather than passively constraining behavior, policies intercept the agent at five structural checkpoints: upstream of planning (Intent Guard), within the system prompt to steer reasoning (Playbook), at the tool-call boundary to enforce proper usage (Tool Guide), outside the reasoning loop as a Human-in-the-Loop gate for high-risk actions (Tool Approvals), and at the output stage to filter and structure the final response (Output Formatter). Together, these stages embed governance continuously across the agent's execution pipeline rather than treating it as an afterthought. Using a healthcare scenario and a multi-layered enforcement intervention, the demo shows dynamic playbook injection for structured tool-sequence enforcement, intent guards that block malicious or accidental harmful requests, and human-in-the-loop tool approval checkpoints for potentially destructive actions. The artifact illustrates how typed governance primitives enable faster, safer deployment of enterprise agentic systems while improving policy adherence and execution consistency.

</details>


### [199] [For How Long Should We Be Punching? Learning Action Duration in Fighting Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20911)
*Hoang Hai Nguyen,Kurt Driessens,Dennis J. N. J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了格斗游戏中让强化学习智能体不仅学习选择动作，还学习动作执行时长（动态帧跳过）的决策框架。实验表明，虽然学习到的时序能匹配固定帧跳过的性能并形成重复动作模式，但智能体倾向于采用高帧跳过（低响应性）的利用性策略，这暴露了脚本对手的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL智能体在格斗游戏中以固定帧率决策，要么帧级完美反应（不拟人），要么间隔过长（响应不足）。作者希望让智能体自主决策动作持续时间，动态调整响应速度以适应不同游戏情境。

Method: 在FightLadder环境中，智能体同时预测动作及其执行时长（帧跳过数），与脚本内置对手对抗。系统性地测试不同帧跳过配置，分析对性能、响应性和学习行为的影响。

Result: 1) 学习到的时序能匹配精心选择的固定帧跳过性能；2) 学习到的时序鼓励重复性动作模式；3) 智能体在持续高帧跳过（低响应性）时表现最佳，因为容易学习重复同一动作的利用性策略，而脚本对手对这种策略缺乏抵抗力。

Conclusion: 动态帧跳过是一种可行的替代方案，但本身不能确保鲁棒性；智能体倾向于低响应性的利用性策略，揭示了脚本对手的弱点，也为更鲁棒的格斗游戏AI设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Fighting games such as Street Fighter II present unique challenges to reinforcement learning (RL) agents due to their fast-paced, real-time nature. In most RL frameworks, agents are hard-coded to make decisions at a fixed interval, typically every frame or every N frames. Although this design ensures timely responses, it restricts the agent's ability to adjust its reaction timing. Acting every frame grants frame-perfect reflexes, which are unrealistic compared to human players, whereas longer fixed intervals reduce computational cost but hinder responsiveness. We consider an alternative decision-making framework in which the agent learns not only what action to take but also for how long to execute it. By jointly predicting both action and duration, the agent can dynamically adapt its responsiveness to different situations in the game. We implement this method using the open-source FightLadder environment with agents trained against scripted built-in bots, systematically testing different frame skip configurations to analyze their influence on performance, responsiveness, and learned behavior. Experiments show that learned timing can match the performance of well-chosen fixed frame skips and encourages repeatable action patterns, but does not ensure robustness on its own. In most cases, we see agents performing best with consistently high frame skip values (i.e., low responsiveness). This strategy makes it easier to learn exploitative strategies where the same action is repeated over and over, which the scripted bots appear to be susceptible to.

</details>


### [200] [Playing Devil's Advocate: Off-the-Shelf Persona Vectors Rival Targeted Steering for Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21006)
*Ishaan Kelkar,Nebras Alam,Vikram Kakaria,Madhur Panwar,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探究了不同人格特征对模型谄媚行为（sycophancy）的影响，发现使用现成的怀疑或审视型人格导向向量可以有效减少谄媚行为，效果达到对比激活添加（CAA）方法的68%至98%，且在用户正确时不会降低准确性。研究还发现谄媚行为更适合理解为一种人格层面属性而非单一可操控方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准谄媚行为缓解方法（CAA）需要标注谄媚与诚实响应对来推导操控方向，本研究探索是否可以使用现成的、非谄媚数据训练的人格操控向量作为替代方案。

Method: 在两种指令微调模型上，使用现成的怀疑/审视型人格操控向量与CAA方法进行对比实验，评估对谄媚行为的缓解效果，并分析其在用户正确时的准确率表现、人格向量的对称性以及几何空间中的独立性。

Result: 怀疑/审视型人格向量可分别达到CAA效果的约68%和98%；与CAA不同，该方法在用户正确时能保持准确性；向 agreeable 人格操控不会镜像增加谄媚行为；在激活空间中，人格向量与谄媚方向大致独立。

Conclusion: 谄媚行为更适合被视为一种人格层面的属性，而非单一可操控的方向。

Abstract: We study the effect of different persona on \textbf{sycophancy}: model's agreement with users even when the user is incorrect. The standard mitigation, Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), derives a steering direction from labelled pairs of sycophantic and honest responses. This study evaluates whether off-the-shelf persona steering vectors, originally developed for general role-playing and not trained on sycophancy data, can serve as an alternative. In two instruction-tuned models, steering toward personas characterised by doubt or scrutiny reduces sycophancy to approximately $68\%$ and $98\%$ of CAA's effect, and, unlike CAA, maintains accuracy when the user is correct. The effect is also asymmetric: steering toward agreeable personas does not produce a mirror increase in sycophancy. Geometrically, the persona vector is largely independent of the direction of sycophancy in activation space. Collectively, these findings suggest that sycophancy is better understood as a persona-level property rather than a single steerable direction. We release our code here: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Sycophancy-Steering-9DF0/.

</details>


### [201] [AutoRPA: Efficient GUI Automation through LLM-Driven Code Synthesis from Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21082)
*Minghao Chen,Xinyi Hu,Zhou Yu,Yufei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRPA是一个将ReAct式LLM智能体的决策逻辑自动蒸馏为鲁棒RPA函数的框架，在保持任务完成能力的同时减少82%-96%的token消耗，显著提升运行时效率和可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在GUI任务中表现优异但ReAct范式重复调用LLM效率低下，传统RPA虽运行时高效但开发和维护需要大量人工成本，两者之间存在效率与自动化程度的鸿沟。

Method: (1) Translator-Builder流水线：translator智能体将硬编码ReAct动作转换为软编码过程，builder智能体通过跨多条轨迹的检索增强生成合成鲁棒RPA函数；(2) 混合修复策略：结合RPA执行与ReAct回退进行迭代精炼，用于代码验证阶段的纠错。

Result: 在多个GUI环境中，AutoRPA生成的RPA函数成功解决相似任务，同时将token使用量减少82%至96%。

Conclusion: AutoRPA有效弥合了LLM智能体灵活性与RPA高效性之间的差距，通过自动蒸馏机制实现了高复用、低成本的GUI任务自动化方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) based agents have demonstrated proficiency in multi-step interactions with graphical user interfaces (GUIs). While most research focuses on improving single-task performance, practical scenarios often involve repetitive GUI tasks for which invoking LLM reasoning repeatedly, i.e., the ReAct paradigm, is inefficient. Prior to LLMs, traditional Robotic Process Automation (RPA) offers runtime efficiency but demands significant manual effort to develop and maintain. To bridge this gap, we propose AutoRPA, a framework that automatically distills the decision logic of ReAct-style agents into robust RPA functions. AutoRPA introduces two core innovations: (1) A translator-builder pipeline, where a translator agent converts hard-coded ReAct actions into soft-coded procedures, and a builder agent synthesizes robust RPA functions via retrieval-augmented generation over multiple trajectories; (2) A hybrid repair strategy during code verification, combining RPA execution with ReAct-based fallback for iterative refinement. Experiments across multiple GUI environments demonstrate that RPA functions generated by AutoRPA successfully solve similar tasks while reducing token usage by 82% to 96%, significantly improving runtime efficiency and reusability.

</details>


### [202] [ScenePilot: Controllable Boundary-Driven Critical Scenario Generation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21168)
*Qiyu Ruan,Yuxuan Wang,He Li,Zhenning Li,Cheng-zhong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScenePilot提出了一种基于可行性与边界驱动的场景生成框架，通过约束多目标强化学习，结合RSS物理可行性评分与在线学习AV风险预测器，生成在物理上可解决但会导致自动驾驶系统失败的边界带场景，在SafeBench上实现了更高碰撞率同时保持物理有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全关键场景生成方法存在两难：要么忽略车辆-道路物理极限产生视觉极端但物理上不可解的碰撞，要么单独强制执行物理可行性或策略可行性导致过度聚焦激进操控或受限于控制器依赖的能力边界。需要一种能生成物理上可解但自动驾驶系统仍会失败的边界场景的方法。

Method: 将场景生成形式化为约束多目标强化学习问题，结合RSS（Responsibility-Sensitive Safety）衍生的物理可行性分数σ与在线学习的AV风险预测器Φ，并引入步骤级可行性感知屏蔽机制，使探索保持在可行性边界附近同时避免不可行伪影。

Result: 在SafeBench上使用多个规划器的实验表明，ScenePilot在保持物理有效性的同时实现了显著更高的碰撞率（+6.2个百分点），并且在这些边界带场景上进行对抗性微调能够持续降低下游碰撞率。

Conclusion: ScenePilot有效弥合了物理可行性与实际自动驾驶系统失败之间的差距，生成的边界带场景可用于对抗性微调以提升系统安全性。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are central to evaluating autonomous driving systems, yet their rarity in naturalistic logs makes simulation-based stress testing indispensable. Most scenario generation methods treat surrounding agents as adversaries, but they either (i) induce failures without explicitly modeling vehicle-road physical limits, yielding visually extreme yet physically unsolvable crashes, or (ii) enforce physical feasibility or policy feasibility in isolation, which can over-focus on aggressive maneuvers or remain tied to a controller-dependent capability boundary. We propose ScenePilot, a feasibility-guided, boundary-driven framework that targets the boundary band: scenarios that are physically solvable in principle yet still cause the deployed autonomy stack to fail. We formulate generation as constrained multi-objective reinforcement learning, combining an RSS-derived physical-feasibility score $σ$ with an online-learned AV-risk predictor $Φ$, and introduce step-level feasibility-aware shielding to keep exploration near the feasibility boundary while avoiding infeasible artifacts. Experiments on SafeBench with multiple planners show that ScenePilot yields substantially higher collision rates (+6.2 percentage points) while preserving physical validity, and that adversarial fine-tuning on these boundary-band scenarios consistently reduces downstream crash rates. The code is available at https://github.com/QiyuRuan/ScenePilot.

</details>


### [203] [Insights Generator: Systematic Corpus-Level Trace Diagnostics for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21347)
*Akshay Manglik,Apaar Shanker,Kaustubh Deshpande,Jason Qin,Yash Maurya,Veronica Chatrath,Vijay S. Kalmath,Levi Lentz,Yuan,Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向LLM智能体执行轨迹的语料级诊断框架，并构建了多智能体系统Insights Generator（IG），通过自动提出和验证假设生成基于证据的洞察报告，实验表明使用IG报告可将人工专家改进的智能体性能提升30.4个百分点，且编码智能体利用IG洞察也获得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体故障诊断仍以人工为主，检查少量执行轨迹、形成临时假设并迭代，这种方式无法捕捉跨轨迹群体涌现的模式，也无法扩展到单个轨迹包含数万token的生产级语料。

Method: 提出Insights Generator（IG）多智能体系统，采用"侦察兵-调查员"架构：侦察兵负责在轨迹语料中提出候选假设，调查员负责验证假设并生成证据支持的洞察报告，最终以自然语言形式呈现系统性行为模式。

Result: 人类专家使用IG报告改进智能体脚手架（scaffold）性能，相比未修改的基线提升了30.4个百分点；编码智能体利用IG洞察获得一致且稳定的增益；在基准测试中，IG的侦察兵-调查员架构在检测覆盖面上与竞品方法相当；领域专家评定IG报告在深度和证据质量方面领先。

Conclusion: IG系统能够自动化地从大规模执行轨迹语料中提取可操作的诊断洞察，有效弥补了人工诊断在规模和系统性上的不足，验证了多智能体架构在LLM智能体调试中的可行性。

Abstract: Diagnosing failures in LLM agents remains largely manual. Practitioners inspect a small subset of execution traces, form ad-hoc hypotheses, and iterate. This process misses patterns that only emerge across trace populations and does not scale to production corpora where individual traces span tens of thousands of tokens. We formalize the problem of corpus-level trace diagnostics. Given a corpus of execution traces, the goal is to produce grounded natural-language insights that characterize systematic behavioral patterns across trace groups, each linked to supporting evidence. We present the Insights Generator (IG), a multi-agent system that answers diagnostic questions by proposing and testing hypotheses across the trace corpus to produce an evidence-backed insights report. We evaluate IG across qualitative and objective dimensions, spanning rubric-based report assessment and downstream performance improvements achieved by implementing IG insights. Human experts using IG reports improve scaffold performance by 30.4pp over the unmodified baseline scaffold, and coding agents leveraging IG-derived insights show consistent and stable gains. Across benchmarks, IG's scout-investigator architecture produces findings comparable in detection coverage to competing approaches, while domain experts rated IG reports as leading depth and evidence quality.

</details>


### [204] [Teaching AI Through Benchmark Construction: QuestBench as a Course-Based Practice for Accountable Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21413)
*Haiyang Shen,Jiuzheng Wang,Taian Guo,Mugeng Liu,Wenchun Jing,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Zhiyang Chen,Weichen Bi,Yudong Han,Xiaoying Bai,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过基准测试构建来教授AI，让学生将学科知识转化为可验证的高难度问题，以此评估AI深度研究系统。所构建的QuestBench包含14个人文社科领域的256个问题，评估显示当前最佳系统GPT-5.5的通过率仅为57.58%，平均通过率仅16.85%，揭示AI在知识工作中的深层缺陷。该方法帮助学生从AI工具的被动使用者转变为AI输出的主动评判者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI教育主要将AI视为生产力工具（如提示、搜索、总结、编码等），缺乏让学生测试AI并理解自身在判断机器产出知识中角色的教学设置。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过基准测试构建这一教学环节，培养学生对AI输出进行批判性评估的能力。

Method: 作者设计了一种基于基准测试构建的教学实践：学生将学科知识转化为专家级、可验证的问题；学生之间互审问题以消除歧义和捷径；使用这些问题构建的QuestBench评估多种AI深度研究系统。该过程让学生直接接触强大的AI工具，同时要求他们明确可信答案应满足的标准。

Result: QuestBench包含256个问题，覆盖14个人文社科领域。在13个评估的深度研究系统中，平均问题级通过率仅为16.85%，表现最佳的GPT-5.5通过率为57.58%。学生设计的任务揭示了当前AI系统的隐藏失败模式，包括遗漏正确查询、来源、术语或证据标准等问题。

Conclusion: 基准测试构建是一种有效的教学实践，能帮助学生将专业知识视为评判AI输出的基础，而非仅作为AI可检索的内容。该方法让学生在AI进入学习和专业工作时保持负责任的知识行动者角色。QuestBench既作为基准测试产物，也作为可复用的课堂教学环节供更广泛的教育实践参考。

Abstract: As AI becomes part of everyday learning, many courses teach students to use it mainly as a productivity tool: how to prompt, search, summarize, write, code, and use tools more efficiently. We argue that AI education also needs a setting in which students learn to test AI and understand their own role in judging machine-produced knowledge. To this end, we introduce a course-based practice that teaches AI through benchmark construction, using deep research systems as a concrete example of AI-era knowledge work. Students turn disciplinary knowledge into verifiable expert-level questions, review one another's designs for ambiguity and shortcuts, and evaluate AI systems on the resulting tasks. This activity gives students direct exposure to a powerful tool while asking them to specify what a trustworthy answer would require. The produced benchmark, QuestBench, consists of 256 questions across 14 humanities and social-science domains. Evaluation on QuestBench shows that student-designed tasks reveal hidden failures in current deep research systems: across thirteen evaluated systems, the mean question-level pass rate is only 16.85%, and the best-performing system, GPT-5.5, reaches a 57.58% pass rate. The failures are educationally useful because they show how fluent, source-backed answers can still miss the right query, source, term, or evidence standard. Reflections from five student contributors suggest that benchmark construction can help students see professional knowledge not only as content AI may retrieve, but as the basis for judging AI outputs. We present QuestBench as a benchmark artifact and as a reusable classroom setting for a larger educational question: how students can remain responsible knowledge actors as AI enters learning and professional work. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/PKUAIWeb/QuestBench/tree/main.

</details>


### [205] [PALS: Power-Aware LLM Serving for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21427)
*Can Hankendi,Rana Shahout,Minlan Yu,Ayse K. Coskun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PALS是一个面向LLM推理的功耗感知运行时系统，将GPU功耗上限作为可调控参数，与批量大小等软件参数联合优化。该系统结合轻量级离线功耗性能模型与反馈驱动控制器，在满足吞吐目标的同时最大化能效。在vLLM框架中实现后，PALS在多GPU系统上实现高达26.3%的能效提升，在功耗约束下将QoS违规降低4-7倍，并能跟踪动态功耗预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理系统主要优化吞吐量和延迟（通过批处理、调度和并行化），但将GPU功耗视为静态约束而非可控资源，未能充分利用功耗调控来提升能效。

Method: 提出PALS系统：(1) 将GPU功耗上限作为一等控制旋钮，与批量大小等软件参数联合优化；(2) 结合轻量级离线功耗性能模型与反馈驱动控制器；(3) 在vLLM框架中实现，无需模型重训练或API变更。

Result: 在多GPU系统及密集和MoE模型上，能效提升高达26.3%，功耗约束下QoS违规降低4-7倍，能跟踪动态功耗预算。

Conclusion: 将功耗控制直接集成到LLM推理运行时中具有显著潜力，可构建能效比例和电网互动的AI系统。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference has become a dominant workload in modern data centers, driving significant GPU utilization and energy consumption. While prior systems optimize throughput and latency by batching, scheduling, and parallelism, they largely treat GPU power as a static constraint rather than a controllable resource. In this paper, we present a power-aware runtime for LLM serving, PALS, that treats GPU power caps as a first-class control knob and jointly optimizes them with software parameters such as batch size. The system combines lightweight offline power-performance models with a feedback-driven controller to select configurations that satisfy throughput targets while maximizing energy efficiency. We implement PALS within an existing LLM serving framework, vLLM, demonstrating that it requires no model retraining or API changes. Across multi-GPU systems and both dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, PALS improves energy efficiency by up to 26.3%, reduces QoS violations by 4x to 7x under power constraints, and tracks dynamic power budgets. These results highlight the potential of integrating power control directly into LLM inference runtimes, enabling energy-proportional and grid-interactive AI systems.

</details>


### [206] [Mind the Sim-to-Real Gap & Think Like a Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21458)
*Harsh Parikh,Gabriel Levin-Konigsberg,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alexander Volfovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在拥有预训练模拟器但存在校准偏差的情况下，如何通过有限真实实验来补充模拟器，以优化序贯决策策略。提出了Fisher-SEP方法，通过最小化目标策略价值的事后预测方差来平衡模拟与实验，并在两个案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟器虽廉价但存在来自校准数据的混杂和漂移（偏差）；真实实验无偏但成本高昂。需要在模拟与实验之间找到最优权衡，即何时、如何用真实实验补充模拟器。

Method: 1) 扩展模拟引理：将模拟器的价值误差分解为校准-部署偏移（可通过随机化识别）和参数残差（不可进一步交互减少）。2) 价值间隙分解：模拟器最优策略与真实最优策略的价值差分为局部成分（已访问状态）和可达性成分（未访问状态），后者在纯被动学习下不会消失。3) 提出Fisher-SEP：最小化目标策略价值的事后预测方差，包含仅奖励和仅转移两种特化版本。

Result: 在自动售货机供应链案例中，当前期实验足够长时，前期实验优于事后更新；在HIV移动检测案例中，只有主动设计探索才能到达监控薄弱的区域。

Conclusion: 模拟器与真实实验的最优结合取决于问题特性：当需要探索未访问状态时，仅靠被动学习无法弥补间隙，必须主动设计实验进行探索。

Abstract: Suppose a planner has a pre-trained simulator of a sequential decision problem and the option to run real experiments in the field. The simulator is cheap to query but inherits confounding and drift from its calibration data. Experimentation is unbiased but consumes one real unit per trial. We study when, and how, the planner should supplement the simulator with experiments. We give three results. First, an extended simulation lemma decomposes the simulator's value error into a calibration--deployment shift that randomization can identify and a parametric residual that no further interaction can reduce. Second, the value gap between the simulator-optimal policy and the optimum splits into a local component, on states the deployed policy already visits, and a reachability component, on states it does not. The reachability component stays bounded away from zero at any horizon under purely passive learning. Third, we propose Fisher-SEP, a simulation-aided experimental policy (SEP) that minimizes the posterior predictive variance of a target policy's value, with reward-only and transition-only specializations. Two case studies illustrate the regimes. In a vending-machine supply chain, front-loaded experimentation overtakes posterior updating once the horizon is long enough to amortize the pilot. In an HIV mobile-testing example with a corridor that separates a well-surveilled region from a poorly-surveilled one, only designed exploration reaches the poorly-surveilled region.

</details>


### [207] [AiraXiv: An AI-Driven Open-Access Platform for Human and AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21481)
*Junshu Pan,Panzhong Lu,Yixuan Weng,Qiyao Sun,Fang Guo,Zijie Yang,Qiji Zhou,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AiraXiv，一个面向AI时代的开放获取论文平台，支持人类和AI科学家共同作为作者和读者，通过AI增强的分析、评审和读者反馈机制，实现论文的持续迭代演化，并通过ICAIS 2025等实际部署验证了其作为快速、包容、可扩展研究基础设施的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学术出版系统难以应对AI时代激增的投稿量、审稿人负担和会议规模，需要一种新的出版范式来提升可扩展性和效率。

Method: 构建AiraXiv平台，基于开放预印本，融合AI增强分析与评审、读者反馈机制；为人类科学家提供交互式UI，为AI科学家提供基于MCP的交互接口。

Result: 通过实际部署（包括作为ICAIS 2025的投稿平台）验证了AiraXiv作为快速、包容、可扩展研究基础设施的可行性。

Conclusion: AiraXiv展示了AI时代下，人类与AI科学家共同参与、论文持续迭代演化的新型出版范式的潜力，可作为传统会议/期刊中心范式的可扩展替代方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) have accelerated the growth of both human-authored and AI-generated research outputs, placing increasing strain on traditional academic publishing systems and challenging the scalability of conference- and journal-centered paradigms amid rising submission volumes, reviewer workload, and venue size. To address these challenges, we explore an AI-era publishing paradigm in which both human and AI scientists participate as authors and readers, and papers evolve through continuous, feedback-driven iteration. We propose AiraXiv, an AI-driven open-access platform built on open preprints, AI-augmented analysis and review, and reader feedback. AiraXiv supports human scientists through an interactive UI and AI scientists through Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based interactions. We validate AiraXiv through real-world deployments, including serving as the submission platform for ICAIS 2025, demonstrating its potential as a fast, inclusive, and scalable research infrastructure for the AI era. AiraXiv is publicly available at https://airaxiv.com.

</details>


### [208] [DeepWeb-Bench: A Deep Research Benchmark Demanding Massive Cross-Source Evidence and Long-Horizon Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21482)
*Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Haiyang Shen,Jiuzheng Wang,Siqi Zhong,Mugeng Liu,Chongyang Pan,Peilun Jia,Baoqing Sun,Xiang Jing,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepWeb-Bench是一个比现有基准更难的大模型深度研究评测基准，通过大规模证据收集、跨源调和和长程多步推导三个维度提升难度，发现检索并非瓶颈（仅占12-14%错误），推导与校准失败占70%以上，且强弱模型在错误类型上存在质性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究产品在已有基准上得分过高，难以区分不同模型的真实能力差异，因此需要构建一个更具挑战性的基准来有效评估前沿模型在深度研究任务上的表现。

Method: 构建DeepWeb-Bench基准，从三个维度提升难度：大规模证据收集、跨源调和、长程多步推导。将能力划分为四个家族（检索、推导、推理、校准），为每个参考答案附带源溯源记录（四级披露+跨源校验）。在9个前沿模型上进行评估。

Result: (1)检索并非瓶颈，检索失败仅占12-14%错误，推导和校准失败占70%以上；(2)强模型和弱模型错误类型存在质性差异，强模型以不完整推导为主，弱模型以幻觉性精确为主；(3)模型存在领域特异性，跨模型一致性仅为rho=0.61，个案分歧达18.8个百分点。

Conclusion: DeepWeb-Bench作为一个更难的深度研究基准，能够有效区分前沿模型能力，揭示了当前模型在推导和校准方面的主要瓶颈，以及不同能力层级模型的差异化失败模式。

Abstract: Deep research, in which an agent searches the open web, collects evidence, and derives an answer through extended reasoning, is a prominent use case for frontier language models. Frontier deep research products score high on existing benchmarks, making it difficult to distinguish their capabilities from current evaluation data alone. We introduce DeepWeb-Bench, a deep research benchmark that is substantially harder than existing benchmarks for the current frontier. Difficulty comes from three properties of the data itself: each task requires massive evidence collection, cross-source reconciliation, and long-horizon multi-step derivation. We represent these three sources of difficulty as four capability families (Retrieval, Derivation, Reasoning, and Calibration) and report results sliced by family. Every reference answer is accompanied by a source-provenance record with four disclosure levels and cross-source checks where available, making scores easier to audit against the underlying evidence. We evaluate DeepWeb-Bench on nine frontier models and report three findings: (1) retrieval is not the bottleneck, as retrieval failures account for only 12-14% of errors while derivation and calibration failures account for over 70%; (2) strong and weak models fail in qualitatively different ways, with strong models' errors dominated by incomplete derivation and weak models' by hallucinated precision; and (3) models exhibit genuine specialization across domains, with cross-model agreement of only rho = 0.61 and per-case disagreement reaching 18.8 percentage points. The public benchmark release includes the data, rubrics, and evaluation code.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Adaptive Human-Robot Collaboration for Masonry Construction Under Material and Assembly Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20264)
*Jutang Gao,Arash Adel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应人机协作砌筑工作流，通过末端投影仪提供空间配准的实时引导，并结合激光扫描反馈驱动抓取与放置位姿修正，有效解决了施工中通信受限和公差累积问题。全尺寸实验表明，投影引导提高了胶粘剂施涂一致性和效率，激光修正维持了水平层并避免了开环执行中的碰撞故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人在协作中面临机器人到人的通信受限以及由材料和装配不确定性导致的公差累积问题。

Method: 提出两种互补机制：1) 末端执行器安装的投影仪，提供空间配准的即时投影引导；2) 激光扫描用于反馈驱动的抓取和放置位姿修正。

Result: 投影引导提高了胶粘剂施涂一致性和效率；激光修正维持了水平层并避免了碰撞故障。

Conclusion: 集成空间投影与基于材料和已建感知的反馈驱动自适应，可以缓解公差累积，提高人机协作施工的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration in construction is often challenged by limited robot-to-human communication and the need to adapt to tolerance accumulation arising from material and assembly uncertainties. We present an adaptive human-robot collaborative workflow for masonry construction that addresses communication limitations and tolerance accumulation, demonstrated through a brickwork case study in which a robot places bricks while a human applies adhesive. This workflow is enabled by two complementary mechanisms: 1) an end-effector-mounted projector that provides spatially registered, just-in-time projection guidance for manual adhesive application, and 2) laser scanning for feedback-driven grasping and placement pose correction. Together, these mechanisms enable adjustment of human and robotic actions in response to material variability and accumulated assembly tolerances. Full-scale experiments across conventional running-bond and nonstandard configurations demonstrate that projection guidance improves adhesive application consistency and reduces application time, while laser-based correction maintains level courses and avoids collision-prone failures associated with open-loop execution. These results indicate that integrating spatial projection with feedback-driven adaptation, enabled by material and as-built sensing, can mitigate tolerance accumulation and improve precision and robustness in human-robot collaborative construction.

</details>


### [210] [Terrestrial Soft Mobile Robots: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20304)
*Dimuthu D. K. Arachchige*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了软体移动机器人的研究现状，聚焦于无轮式陆地运动系统，涵盖运动策略、驱动方法、建模方法及控制系统，并指出了关键研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体移动机器人在搜索救援、服务、监视、勘探和制造等多个领域具有广阔应用前景，但尚缺乏对其无轮式陆地运动系统的系统性综述，因此需要进行全面回顾以推动该领域发展。

Method: 采用综合性文献综述方法，系统梳理了软体移动机器人在运动策略、驱动方法、建模方法和控制系统方面的过去与现有发展。

Result: 归纳了软体移动机器人在无轮式陆地运动方面的关键技术进展，识别出阻碍其广泛应用的若干关键研究挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为软体机器人和软体移动机器人领域的研究者和从业者提供了有价值的参考资料，有助于推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Soft mobile robots have emerged as a promising area of research with potential applications in various disciplines including but not limited to search-and-rescue, service, surveillance, explorations, and manufacturing. In this article, we provide a comprehensive review of the current state of soft mobile robot research, focusing on wheelless terrestrial locomotive systems. We include past and present developments in locomotion strategies, actuation methods, modeling approaches, and control systems. Further, we identify key research challenges that must be overcome to enable the widespread adoption of soft mobile robots in various applications. Overall, this article provides a valuable resource for researchers and practitioners interested in the field of soft mobile robots and soft robotics.

</details>


### [211] [Proximal State Nudging: Reducing Skill Atrophy from AI Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20355)
*Megha Srivastava,Jonathan Ouyang,Eric Zhou,Andrew Silva,Emily Sumner,Dorsa Sadigh,Yuchen Cui,Deepak Gopinath,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出近端状态引导（PSN）算法，在共享自主系统中联合优化技能发展与任务表现，通过引导用户走向最可学习的状态来防止技能萎缩。在LunarLander模拟实验和CARLA驾驶模拟器中的人类实验（n=60）表明，PSN在无辅助技能提升上优于现有基线，最高提升7倍，且碰撞减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享控制半自主系统中，人在AI辅助下可能出现技能萎缩（能力逐渐下降），导致紧急情况下无法区分自身输入与自主修正，构成安全隐患。现有方法多聚焦任务性能，忽略了对人类技能发展的维护。

Method: 提出Proximal State Nudging (PSN)算法，通过将用户引导（nudge）至估计最可学习的状态，在共享控制中联合优化技能发展与任务表现。首先用模拟学生在LunarLander环境验证，再在CARLA模拟器中开展两项驾驶任务（高性能赛车和侧方停车）的人类受试者实验（n=60）。

Result: PSN在无辅助技能增益上比标准混合共享自主方法高出最多7倍，比无辅助自学减少50%的碰撞次数。

Conclusion: PSN证明了在共享自主系统中纳入学习兼容性设计可以有效缓解技能萎缩，同时保持良好的任务安全表现，为半自主系统的人机协作提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Skill atrophy, the gradual decline of human capability under AI assistance, poses a safety risk in shared-control of semi-autonomous systems, where operators may be unable to distinguish their own inputs from autonomous corrections. We propose Proximal State Nudging (PSN), a shared autonomy algorithm that jointly optimizes for skill development and task performance by nudging users toward states estimated to be most learnable. We first show that PSN outperforms existing shared autonomy baselines in balancing student improvement in unassisted reward with overall shared performance, using simulated students in the classic LunarLander environment. We then present, to the best of our knowledge, the first human subject studies of a planner incorporating learning-compatible shared autonomy: across two driving tasks in the CARLA simulator (High Performance Racing and Parallel Parking, n = 60), PSN produces up to 7x larger gains in unassisted skill than standard blended shared autonomy, while incurring 50% fewer collisions than unassisted self-practice.

</details>


### [212] [SUGAR: A Scalable Human-Video-Driven Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20373)
*Tianshu Wu,Xiangqi Kong,Yue Chen,Qize Yu,Hang Ye,Jia Li,Yizhou Wang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUGAR是一个可扩展的数据驱动框架，能从人类视频中自动提取运动先验，通过物理精炼和知识蒸馏，生成可部署于真实人形机器人的全身操作技能，无需任务级奖励工程或参考运动条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖繁琐的任务级奖励工程、刚性回放参考运动导致泛化能力差，或依赖昂贵的遥操作难以扩展。人类视频虽包含多样行为，但从中推断的运动先验存在遮挡、接触伪影和重定向误差，无法直接用于策略学习。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 从非结构化人类视频中自动提取运动交互先验（人体-物体运动轨迹与接触标签）；(2) 基于特权信息的物理精炼器，使用统一模仿奖励和渐进状态池，将不完美先验转化为物理可行、高保真的技能；(3) 将精炼技能蒸馏为分层自主策略（命令生成器+命令跟踪器）。

Result: 在六个代表性全身操作任务上，SUGAR显著优于参考跟踪基线，性能随人类视频数据量增加而明确提升。零样本迁移到真实世界，实现可靠闭环执行、自主故障恢复和长时程稳定操作。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了利用大规模人类视频数据学习可泛化的人形机器人全身操作技能的可行性，为摆脱人工奖励工程和遥操作依赖提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Building humanoid robots capable of generalizable whole-body loco-manipulation in the real world remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods either rely on laborious task-specific reward engineering, rigidly replay reference motions that fail to generalize, or depend on costly teleoperation that limits scalability. While human videos capture diverse human behaviors, motion priors inferred from them are inherently imperfect, suffering from occlusion, contact artifacts, and retargeting errors that render them unsuitable for direct policy learning. To address this, we present SUGAR, a scalable data-driven framework that converts diverse human videos into deployable humanoid loco-manipulation skills, without any task-specific reward engineering or reference-motion conditioning at inference. SUGAR proceeds in three stages. First, a fully automated pipeline extracts kinematic interaction priors including human-object motion trajectories and contact labels from unstructured human videos. Second, a privileged physics-based refiner uses a unified mimic reward and progressive state pool to transform imperfect priors into physically feasible, high-fidelity skills. Third, refined skills are distilled into a hierarchical autonomous policy consisting of a command generator and a command tracker. We evaluate SUGAR on six representative loco-manipulation tasks in simulation and real-world humanoid hardware. Our method substantially outperforms reference-tracking baselines, and performance scales clearly with the amount of human video data. It also achieves zero-shot real-world transfer with reliable closed-loop execution, autonomous failure recovery, and stable long-horizon performance under external perturbations. Project Page: https://tianshuwu.github.io/sugar-humanoid/

</details>


### [213] [Scalable Multi-robot Motion Planning via Hierarchical Subproblem Expansion and Workspace Decomposition Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20395)
*Isaac Ngui,Courtney McBeth,James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多机器人运动规划方法，通过工作空间分解的离散搜索来提供机器人间的协调，比联合构型空间搜索快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划的核心挑战在于：既要实现足够的协调以避免机器人间冲突，又要避免联合构型空间搜索带来的巨大计算开销。

Method: 利用工作空间分解上的离散搜索来提供规划过程中机器人间的协调；与先前工作相比，进一步迭代细化工作空间表示，使得规划器能够搜索更小、解耦的构型空间。

Result: 规划时间提升最高达一个数量级（约10倍）。

Conclusion: 通过工作空间分解的离散搜索提供协调，并迭代细化表示以搜索解耦构型空间，是一种高效的多机器人运动规划方法。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in multi-robot motion planning is achieving sufficient coordination to avoid inter-robot conflicts without incurring the large computational expense of searching the joint configuration space of the robot group. In this work, we present a method for multiple mobile robot motion planning that achieves an improvement in planning time up to an order of magnitude by leveraging the insight that we can use discrete search over a workspace decomposition to provide coordination between robots during planning. While prior work uses workspace topology to inform when coordination between robots is needed and then composes robots into their joint configuration space, we take a step further by iteratively refining our workspace representation to allow our planner to search smaller, decoupled configuration spaces.

</details>


### [214] [Enhancing Graph-Based SLAM in GNSS-Denied environments by leveraging leg odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20484)
*Léon Perruchot-Triboulet,Luc Jaulin,Kai Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因子图架构，在LIO-SAM框架中并联腿部里程计（本体感知）作为垂直约束，将四足机器人在无GNSS环境下的高程漂移从30米以上降至30厘米以下，并在基线方法完全失效的场景中实现了收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无GNSS环境中，四足机器人依赖的激光雷达等外感受传感器在几何稀疏或重复场景中容易出现高程漂移，而现有的LIO-SAM框架无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 在LIO-SAM因子图框架中引入一条并行的腿部里程计（本体感知）因子链，通过与主激光雷达-惯性因子链之间建立带选择性噪声模型的恒等相对位姿约束，实现两类信息的融合。

Result: 在Linxai D50四足平台上，经过两条总长超1公里的户外环路测试，高程漂移从30米以上降至30厘米以下，且在基线方法完全失败的场景中仍能收敛。

Conclusion: 本体感知数据（已用于步态控制）可作为一种轻量且有效的垂直锚点，用于无GNSS环境下的SLAM系统，大幅提升高程估计的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in GNSS-denied environments remains a core challenge for legged robots, where exteroceptive sensors such as LiDAR are prone to elevation drift in geometrically sparse or repetitive scenes. We present a factor graph architecture that augments the LIO-SAM framework with a parallel kinematic lane driven by proprioceptive leg odometry, coupled to the main LiDAR-inertial lane via an identity relative pose constraint with a selective noise model. Applied to a Linxai D50 quadruped platform across two outdoor loops totaling over one kilometer, our approach reduces elevation drift from over 30m to under 30cm and enables convergence in a scene where the baseline pipeline fails entirely. These results suggest that proprioceptive data, already computed onboard for gait control, constitutes a lightweight and effective vertical anchor for SLAM in GNSS-denied settings.

</details>


### [215] [The Yes-Man Syndrome: Benchmarking Abstention in Embodied Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20544)
*Doguhan Yeke,Elif Su Temirel,Ananth Shreekumar,Brandon Lee,Dongyan Xu,Z Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboAbstention框架，用于评估和改善VLM在具身机器人规划中的弃权能力。构建了包含6069条指令的基准，发现最佳模型仅能正确弃权39.0%，通过防御性提示和上下文学习可将弃权率提升至93.6%，但问题尚未完全解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM弃权研究主要针对纯文本场景，缺乏对具身机器人环境中感知基础（perceptual grounding）和物理约束的考虑。在机器人场景中，弃权需要识别指令是否模糊、物理上不可行、基于错误前提或无法通过现有感官模态解决，而这方面缺乏系统性的基准和评估。

Method: 提出RoboAbstention框架：(1) 构建具身机器人弃权分类法（taxonomy）；(2) 设计三阶段流水线：结构化视觉基础、确定性约束推导、基于类别模板的受控指令生成；(3) 从5个机器人数据集的图像中生成可验证的弃权指令数据集；(4) 评估多个前沿VLM并用防御性提示和上下文学习提升性能。

Result: 在6069条基准指令中，最佳模型Gemini 2.5 Flash仅正确弃权39.0%，具身规划器Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview仅16.5%。通过防御性提示和上下文学习，Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview弃权率提升至93.6%，GPT 5.4 Mini提升至88.6%，但均未完全解决问题。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在具身机器人规划中弃权能力严重不足，即便具备高级推理能力的模型也存在明显弱点；防御性提示和上下文学习可显著改善但无法完全解决，表明该领域仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are used as high-level planners for embodied agents, translating natural language instructions and visual observations into action plans. While prior work has studied abstention in LLMs, existing benchmarks are largely text-only and do not capture the perceptual grounding and physical constraints inherent to embodied robotics environments. In such settings, abstention requires recognizing when instructions are ambiguous, physically infeasible, based on false premises, or otherwise unresolvable given the available sensory modalities and context. To address this gap, we introduce a taxonomy to categorize abstention in the context of embodied robotics and present RoboAbstention, a scalable and auditable framework for generating abstention instructions grounded in images gathered from five robotics datasets. RoboAbstention instantiates the taxonomy through a three-phase pipeline: (1) structured visual grounding, (2) deterministic constraint derivation, and (3) controlled instruction generation via category-specific templates. This enables the construction of a diverse dataset with verifiable abstention conditions. We evaluate several frontier VLMs and find that all models exhibit significant weaknesses in abstention, including those with advanced reasoning capabilities. The best-performing model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, abstains on only 39.0% of our 6,069 benchmark instructions, while the embodied planner Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview abstains on just 16.5%. We further explore methods for improving abstention in VLM planners, such as defensive prompting and in-context learning, and find that these interventions substantially improve performance, reaching 93.6% abstention rate for Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview and 88.6% for GPT 5.4 Mini, yet no approach fully solves the problem. We open-source RoboAbstention at https://purseclab.github.io/RoboAbstention/.

</details>


### [216] [Fault-Tolerant, Rigidity-Preserving Control of Inflatable Truss Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20561)
*James Wade,Isaac Weaver,Mihai Stanciu,Nathan Usevitch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对充气式等周机器人桁架在执行器故障下的容错控制问题，提出了包含运动学优化扩展、离散时间控制障碍函数约束和闭环位置控制三方面贡献的容错控制框架，在2D等周桁架试验台上验证了单电机故障下>69%的工作空间保持率和>25%的跟踪精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等周机器人桁架在运行环境中可能发生电机故障，这会严重限制其操作能力，而现有方法未充分解决这一问题，因此需要开发一种能够维持故障下功能的容错控制框架。

Method: 首先，通过施加等式约束确保故障执行器不被使用，将运动学优化扩展到任意电机故障组合；其次，引入离散时间控制障碍函数（DTCBF）约束，在离散时间控制下数学保证结构刚性并最大化工作空间利用率；最后，利用板载编码器反馈和基于正向运动学的状态估计器实现闭环位置控制，提高存在扰动时的定位精度。

Result: 在2D等周桁架试验台（6个执行器）上，单电机故障下工作空间保持率>69%，闭环控制下跟踪精度提升>25%。

Conclusion: 该容错控制框架为在退化驱动条件下运行更鲁棒、更具韧性的等周桁架机器人建立了基础。

Abstract: Isoperimetric robotic trusses can adapt to different tasks and environments because they have a high strength-to-weight ratio, can change their own shape dramatically, and can be reconfigured into a variety of different shapes. However, motor failures in operational environments can severely limit operational capabilities if not properly addressed. This paper presents a fault-tolerant control framework for an inflatable robotic truss that maintains functionality despite motor failures, shown through three key contributions. First, we extend the kinematic optimization to handle arbitrary combinations of motor failures by imposing equality constraints to ensure failed actuators are not used. Second, we introduce discrete-time control barrier function (DTCBF) constraints that mathematically guarantee structural rigidity while maximizing workspace utilization, a critical requirement for reliable operation of truss robots under discrete-time control. Third, we implement closed-loop position control using onboard encoder feedback and a forward kinematics-based state estimator, improving positional accuracy in the presence of disturbances. We validate our approach through simulation and hardware experiments on a 2D isoperimetric truss testbed. For a 2D configuration with 6 actuators, we demonstrate >69% workspace preservation under single-motor failures and a >25% improvement in tracking accuracy with closed-loop control. These results establish a foundation for more robust and resilient isoperimetric truss robots operating under degraded actuation.

</details>


### [217] [Conflict-Aware Active Perception and Control in 3D Gaussian Splatting Fields via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20566)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Vivek Pandey,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO
