TL;DR: 本文提出了一种冲突感知的主动感知与控制框架，利用3D高斯溅射（3DGS）表示环境，通过控制障碍函数（CBF）保障安全，并结合风险感知的期望信息增益（EIG）选择最佳视点，最终通过安全关键、感知感知的二次规划（QP）统一处理安全与感知目标的冲突。仿真结果表明该方法在安全性和信息获取方面均优于现有3DGS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定环境中，机器人需要在安全导航与获取信息以减少地图不确定性之间做出权衡，因为信息量大的视点往往位于碰撞风险较高的不确定区域附近，这两个目标本质上是冲突的。

Method: 1) 使用基于平均风险值（AV@R）碰撞风险度量的控制障碍函数（CBF）保障安全，确保安全集的前向不变性；2) 提出风险感知的期望信息增益（EIG）公式用于选择下一最佳视点，并引入感知障碍函数使相机朝向与局部信息上升方向对齐；3) 构建统一的安全关键、感知感知的二次规划（QP），将安全作为硬约束，感知通过松弛变量作为软约束。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在安全性和信息获取方面均优于现有的基于3DGS的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将安全作为硬约束、感知作为软约束的统一QP框架，可以有效协调主动感知中安全与信息获取的冲突目标，提升机器人系统在不确定环境中的整体性能。

Abstract: Active perception in uncertain environments requires robots to navigate safely while acquiring informative observations to reduce map uncertainty. These objectives inherently conflict, as informative viewpoints often lie near uncertain regions with higher collision risk. To address this challenge, we develop a conflict-aware active perception and control framework for robotic systems operating in environments represented by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Safety is enforced using a Control Barrier Function (CBF) derived from an Average Value-at-Risk AV@R collision-risk metric that accounts for geometric uncertainty and guarantees forward invariance of a safe set. To improve perception, we propose a risk-aware Expected Information Gain (EIG) formulation for selecting the next-best-view and introduce perception barrier functions that align the camera orientation with the local information-ascent direction. To obtain a tractable formulation for these conflicting safety and perception objectives, we propose a unified safety-critical, perception-aware quadratic program that enforces safety as a hard constraint while relaxing perception constraints through slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method improves both safety and information acquisition compared to existing 3DGS-based approaches.

</details>


### [218] [Intent-First Aerial V2V for Tactical Coordination and Separation: Protocol and Performance Under Density and Disturbance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20595)
*Mehrnaz Sabet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个全空中、侧链路类、意图优先的无人机V2V战术邻域交换协议栈，通过结合状态/意图信标与事件触发消息实现战术间隔管理。评估表明该方案在低至中密度场景下有效，但在高密度、高干扰条件下需回归安全回退机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低空密集无人机运行中，预飞航线协调反应太慢、碰撞避免又过于激进，缺乏中间层的战术间隔管理机制。需要一种可部署的邻域通信机制，提供新鲜、可信的信息支持局部协调。

Method: 提出意图优先的全空中V2V战术邻域交换协议栈，包括刷新状态/意图信标（用于局部感知、协同感知、退化模式评估）和事件触发消息（用于让行、排序、释放、应急协调）。使用侧链路类C-V2X模块实现，并基于实时场测基础设施进行高压力场景测试。

Result: V2V减少了陈旧信念发散，通过协同感知保持可观测性，拒绝无效战术消息，抑制虚假局部推断，并结构化共享资源协调。在低至中密度场景下表现良好，但在密度、干扰和复杂度增加时需转为有防护的安全回退。

Conclusion: 意图优先的空中V2V是扩展城市扰动驱动空域中战术协调的有限使能器，为无人机交通管理提供可行的通信层方案。

Abstract: Dense low-altitude aerial operations require more than pre-flight route coordination and last-resort collision avoidance. Once aircraft are airborne, disturbances can emerge on timescales shorter than strategic reauthorization can absorb, while collision avoidance is too late and disruptive to serve as routine traffic management. Although tactical separation is recognized as the intermediate layer, realizing it at scale requires a deployable neighborhood communication mechanism that provides fresh, trusted information for local coordination. This paper presents what is, to our knowledge, the first controller-coupled characterization of an all-airborne, sidelink-class, intent-first vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) tactical neighborhood exchange stack for dense Unmanned Aircraft System Traffic Management (UTM) operations. Unlike awareness-only broadcast, the proposed exchange combines refreshed state and intent beacons for local awareness, cooperative perception, and degraded-mode assessment with event-triggered messages for yielding, sequencing, release, and contingency coordination. We implement and evaluate this model on an all-airborne V2V stack using sidelink-class C-V2X modules with authenticated freshness checks. Evaluation uses a scenario-driven, high-volume stress campaign supported by real-time, field-anchored infrastructure. Results show that V2V reduces stale-belief divergence, preserves observability through cooperative perception, rejects invalid tactical messages, suppresses false local inference, and structures shared-resource coordination. The implemented stack provides a viable communication layer for tactical separation in lower-to-moderate regimes, but transitions toward guarded fallback as density, impairment, and complexity increase. These findings position intent-first aerial V2V as a bounded enabler for scaling tactical coordination in disturbance-driven urban airspace.

</details>


### [219] [Jointly Learning Predicates and Actions Enables Zero-Shot Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20648)
*Benedict Quartey,Sebastian Castro,Eric Rosen,Wil Thomason,George Konidaris,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Predicate Action Skills (PACTS)，一种联合建模动作轨迹与符号谓词信念的生成式策略，实现技能零样本组合与执行监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法无法泛化到已知技能的零样本组合，且生成式策略仅建模动作轨迹，无法推理符号结果，缺乏鲁棒组合能力。

Method: 提出PACTS，一种闭环视觉运动策略，将技能建模为动作与谓词信念轨迹的联合生成过程，通过联合生成学习内部表征提升动作生成与谓词分类，并利用在线谓词预测作为符号接口进行规划与执行监控。

Result: 实现零样本组合学习到的技能，通过规划的符号接口实现技能序列化与执行监控。

Conclusion: 联合建模动作与符号谓词信念可提升生成策略的组合泛化能力，为机器人技能复用与零样本组合提供新范式。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) enables robots to learn complex behaviors from expert examples, yet existing approaches often fail to generalize to new compositions of known skills without retraining. Modern generative policies model distributions over action trajectories alone, thus are unable to reason about the symbolic outcomes required for robust composition. We propose that skills should jointly model action trajectories and the symbolic outcomes they induce. To address this gap, we introduce Predicate Action Skills (PACTS), a class of closed-loop visuomotor policies that model skills as a joint generative process over action and predicate belief trajectories, producing coherent action-outcome rollouts within a single model. Jointly generating actions and predicates enables PACTS to learn internal representations that improve both action generation and predicate classification. Furthermore, we demonstrate zero-shot composition of learned skills via planning by leveraging online predicate predictions from PACTS as a symbolic interface for sequencing and monitoring execution. Project website: https://planpacts.github.io/

</details>


### [220] [A Semantic and Occlusion-Aware GM-PHD Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20666)
*Jovan Menezes,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度学习语义信息与遮挡感知的新型出生模型（S-OA），用于高斯混合概率假设密度（GM-PHD）滤波器，在复杂高密度驾驶场景中显著提升追踪性能，在约70%的案例中匹配或超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GM-PHD滤波器中的出生模型通常采用简单或均匀的假设，无法有效处理遮挡区域中新目标的出现，导致在复杂高密度驾驶场景下追踪性能不佳。因此，需要一种能够感知遮挡并利用语义信息的出生模型来提高追踪精度和初始化效率。

Method: 提出Semantic-Occlusion Aware (S-OA)出生模型，利用深度学习提取语义信息，显式考虑遮挡区域来定义初始化项，使滤波器能更准确地表示新目标可能出现的位置，并将该模型集成到GM-PHD滤波器中。

Result: 通过蒙特卡洛仿真和KITTI数据集实验评估，以首次检测与轨迹初始化之间的延迟、平均绝对基数误差和OSPA指标衡量性能。结果表明S-OA模型在遮挡严重场景下减少了初始化延迟，约70%案例匹配或超越最强基线，并提供了出生模型权重的敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 将遮挡推理和语义先验融入贝叶斯追踪框架可显著提升自主驾驶场景中的追踪性能，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new birth model including semantic information derived from deep learning to create an occlusion-aware Gaussian Mixture Probability Hypothesis Density (GM-PHD) filter. Unlike prior approaches that rely on simplistic or uniform assumptions, the proposed Semantic-Occlusion Aware (S-OA) birth model defines initialization terms by explicitly considering regions of occlusion and by leveraging semantic information about the environment. This enables the filter to accurately represent where new objects are more likely to appear, thereby improving tracking performance in complex and high-density driving scenarios. The method is evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations and experiments on the KITTI dataset. Performance is assessed by measuring the latency between first detection and track initiation, along with the mean absolute cardinality error and the Optimal Subpattern Assignment (OSPA) metric. Results demonstrate that the S-OA birth model reduces initialization delay in occlusion-heavy settings, matching or outperforming the strongest baseline in approximately 70% of cases. A sensitivity analysis of birth model weights is also provided. Overall, the findings underscore the benefits of integrating occlusion reasoning and semantic priors into Bayesian tracking frameworks for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [221] [GaussianDream: A Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20752)
*Zijian Zhang,Yuqing Jiang,Qian Cheng,Si Liu,Ding Zhao,Ping Luo,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianDream是一种前馈式3D高斯世界模型插件，通过将机器人轨迹转化为结构化的时空监督信号，在训练时耦合当前高斯重建与未来高斯预测，迫使紧凑的时空前缀可解码为可渲染的3D高斯状态，从而提供密集的RGB渲染、深度和伪3D场景流监督，推理时仅保留学习到的前缀来条件化动作生成，在LIBERO、RoboCasa Human-50和真实机器人任务上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的动作模仿训练对3D几何、密集视觉结构和短视距环境演化的显式监督不足，而这些对于物理精确的操作至关重要。

Method: 提出GaussianDream，一种前馈式3D高斯世界模型插件：训练时将当前高斯重建与水平条件化的未来高斯预测耦合，迫使紧凑的时空前缀可解码为可渲染的3D高斯状态，提供密集RGB渲染、深度和伪3D场景流监督；推理时丢弃所有辅助解码头，仅保留学习到的前缀来条件化动作生成，无需渲染、视频回滚或额外规划。

Result: LIBERO上平均成功率98.4%，RoboCasa Human-50上52.6%，真实世界评估中50.0%。

Conclusion: GaussianDream通过引入时空结构化监督显著提升了VLA策略在物理精确操作任务上的性能，且推理时无需额外计算开销，是一种高效且有效的世界模型插件方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation by transferring semantic priors from pretrained vision-language models to action generation. Yet, standard action-imitation training often provides limited explicit supervision for 3D geometry, dense visual structure, and short-horizon environment evolution, which are critical for physically precise manipulation. We introduce \textbf{GaussianDream}, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian world-model plug-in that turns robot trajectories into structured spatial-temporal supervision. The key idea is to couple current Gaussian reconstruction with horizon-conditioned future Gaussian prediction during training, forcing a compact spatio-temporal prefix to be decodable into renderable 3D Gaussian states. This enables dense RGB rendering, depth, and pseudo 3D scene-flow supervision without requiring test-time Gaussian decoding. At inference, GaussianDream discards all auxiliary decoding heads and retains only the learned prefix to condition action generation, avoiding rendering, video rollout, or additional planning during closed-loop control. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa Human-50, and real-robot tasks demonstrate strong and highly competitive performance, achieving \textbf{98.4\%} average success on LIBERO, \textbf{52.6\%} on RoboCasa Human-50, and \textbf{50.0\%} in real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [222] [VLA-REPLICA: A Low-Cost, Reproducible Benchmark for Real-World Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20774)
*Alex S. Huang,Jiahui Zhang,Shiqing Tang,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-REPLICA是一个低成本、易复现的具身智能基准测试平台，用于评估视觉-语言-动作模型在真实世界中的机器人操作能力，支持跨实验室的一致环境复现和多样化任务评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仿真基准无法捕捉真实世界的复杂性，而真实世界基准又存在硬件成本高、评估中心化、任务多样性有限等问题，缺乏一个既低成本又易于复现的标准化评估平台。

Method: 基于现成商用组件搭建低成本硬件系统，支持快速组装和跨实验室复现；设计了多样化的操作任务套件和面向目标域适应的小规模演示数据集；制定了面向分布内和分布外场景的真实世界评估协议。

Result: 通过模仿学习与最先进的VLA模型实验揭示了模型的优势与局限性；在独立搭建的不同实验装置上获得了一致的结果，验证了平台的复现性。

Conclusion: VLA-REPLICA为VLA模型的真实世界评估提供了一个可访问、可复现且一致的基准平台，弥补了仿真与现有真实世界基准之间的不足。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but their real-world evaluation remains limited by a lack of accessible, reproducible, and consistent benchmarks. Simulation benchmarks fail to capture real-world complexity, while existing real-world benchmarks often require expensive hardware, centralized evaluation, or are limited in task diversity. We introduce VLA-REPLICA, a low-cost, easily reproducible real-world benchmark for evaluating VLA models. Built from off-the-shelf components, our system can be quickly assembled and replicated across laboratories, providing a consistent environment for policy evaluation anywhere in the world. VLA-REPLICA includes a diverse suite of manipulation tasks and a small-scale demonstration dataset for target-domain adaptation, with real-world evaluation protocols for both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Experiments with imitation learning and state-of-the-art VLA models reveal model strengths and limitations, while consistent results across independently constructed setups demonstrate the reproducibility of our benchmark.

</details>


### [223] [CMC-Opt: Constraint Manifold with Corners for Inequality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20796)
*Yetong Zhang,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于流形的框架，将带有等式和不等式约束的机器人优化问题转化为在约束状态空间上的无约束优化问题。通过引入"带角的约束流形"来表示满足混合非线性等式和不等式约束的状态空间，并扩展流形优化算法以适应这种新的拓扑结构。在大规模动力学规划问题中，该方法成功生成了标准方法无法实现的动态可行轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域中存在大量带有等式和不等式约束的优化问题，标准优化方法在处理这类混合约束时常常失败，尤其是在大规模动力学规划问题中。现有方法缺乏一种能够统一处理混合约束并将其转化为无约束问题的有效框架。

Method: 提出"带角的约束流形"概念，用于表示满足混合非线性等式和不等式约束的状态空间；将原始约束优化问题转化为约束状态空间上的无约束优化问题；扩展流形优化算法使其能够在该新拓扑结构上运行。

Result: 在大规模动力学规划问题中，该框架成功生成了动态可行的轨迹，而标准方法在此类问题上失败。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于流形的框架能够有效处理机器人中的混合等式和不等式约束优化问题，在标准方法失败的大规模问题上表现出鲁棒性和有效性，为复杂约束优化问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: We introduce a manifold-based framework for addressing optimization problems with equality and inequality constraints found in robotics. Our approach transforms the original problem into an unconstrained optimization problem directly on the constrained state space. To achieve this, we introduce ``constraint manifolds with corners" to represent the state space satisfying mixed nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. We further extend manifold optimization algorithms to operate on this new topological structure. We demonstrate the power and robustness of our framework in the context of a large-scale kinodynamic planning problem, successfully generating dynamically feasible trajectories where standard methods fail.

</details>


### [224] [Demo-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for One-shot Cross-Embodiment Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20811)
*Jingyang He,Guangrun Li,Jieyu Zhang,Chengkai Hou,Zhengping Che,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Demo-JEPA是一种基于JEPA世界模型的跨具身模仿学习框架，通过将演示视为隐式未来目标规范，解耦演示意图与具身执行，利用共享预测表征空间将源视觉演示转化为目标兼容的潜在轨迹，再通过目标智能体自身的规划实现，从而在不同形态、运动学或动作空间的智能体间实现灵活的模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习将动作复现作为核心，但动作本质上是具身特有的。当演示来自不同形态、运动学或动作空间的人或机器人时，基于动作的方法需要共享动作空间、启发式重定向或大规模多具身协同训练，效率低下且泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出Demo-JEPA框架，基于JEPA世界模型构建共享预测表征空间，将源视觉演示解码为目标兼容的未来潜在轨迹（子目标序列），目标智能体通过自身的正向动力学模型进行规划来实现这些子目标，完全避免动作级别的对应关系。

Result: 在RLBench和真实世界操作任务上，Demo-JEPA能够匹配专门的领域内规划器，并且在先前方法失败的新任务和具身配置上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Demo-JEPA通过解耦演示意图与具身执行，仅需视觉演示和目标智能体的交互经验即可实现跨异质具身的灵活模仿学习，展示了基于目标而非动作的模仿范式在跨具身泛化中的优势。

Abstract: Robotic imitation learning is often treated as reproducing demonstrated actions, but actions are inherently embodiment-specific. When demonstrations come from humans or robots with different morphology, kinematics, or action spaces, this action-centric view requires shared action spaces, heuristic retargeting, or large-scale multi-embodiment co-training. We instead view demonstrations as implicit specifications of future goals: the target agent should infer what state the demonstrator is trying to realize, rather than how the demonstrator executes it. We propose Demo-JEPA, a cross-embodiment imitation framework that decouples demonstration intent from embodiment-specific execution. Built on a JEPA-based world model, Demo-JEPA translates source visual demonstrations into target-compatible future latent trajectories in a shared predictive representation space. The target agent then uses these latent trajectories as subgoals and realizes them through planning under its own learned forward dynamics. Because Demo-JEPA avoids action-level correspondence and requires only visual demonstrations plus the target agent's own interaction experience, it supports flexible imitation across heterogeneous embodiments. Experiments on RLBench and real-world manipulation tasks show that Demo-JEPA matches specialized in-domain planners and generalizes to unseen tasks and embodiment configurations where prior methods fail.

</details>


### [225] [Mobile UMI: Cross-View Diffusion Policy with Decoupled Kinematics for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20894)
*Haoran Huang,Haonan Dong,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Mobile UMI，一种无硬件的移动模仿学习演示框架。通过双摄像头采集系统（胸部全局视角+手腕局部视角）、基于ChArUco的单次空间锚定实现手部相对胸部运动解耦（分离SE(3)操作与SE(2)基座轨迹）、以及异步滚动时域执行器进行在线状态匹配（丢弃过期路径点），解决了移动基座上的动作标签污染和推理延迟两大瓶颈。在四个长时家务任务中取得83.8%平均成功率，对比实验表明显式运动学分解与状态级延迟对齐的组合不改变底层策略架构即可有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动模仿学习面临两大耦合瓶颈：一是基座运动污染动作标签（人行走时手部运动与基座运动纠缠），二是生成式策略推理延迟导致基座在动作生成期间越过预期路径点，产生反向修正行为。现有解决方案各有不足：手腕安装相机无法捕捉导航所需的全局上下文，添加身体相机则使行走与手部运动纠缠在一起。亟需一种无硬件的、能解耦操作与导航信号并消除延迟影响的演示框架。

Method: Mobile UMI包含三个核心组件：1) 双摄像头采集系统：胸部相机捕获全局上下文，手腕相机捕获局部交互，无需机器人本体参与；2) ChArUco单次空间锚定：统一胸部与手腕的视觉-惯性坐标系，将手部姿态相对于胸部重新表达，提取解耦的SE(3)操作轨迹和SE(2)基座轨迹；3) 异步滚动时域执行器：在线状态匹配，每个生成的动作块与当前物理姿态重新对齐，丢弃已过期的路径点后再执行。

Result: 在四个长时家务任务上各测试100次，平均成功率达83.8%。与ACT和Diffusion Policy的对照实验表明：仅使用胸部相对标签即可缩小大部分性能差距，在线状态匹配则填补剩余差距。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，显式运动学分解（将操作与导航信号解耦）结合状态级延迟对齐，无需改变底层策略架构即可有效解决移动模仿学习中的动作污染和推理延迟问题。

Abstract: Mobile imitation learning on portable demonstration interfaces faces two coupled bottlenecks: locomotion-contaminated action labels and inference-induced execution latency on a continuously moving base. Recent wrist-mounted interfaces lower the cost of tabletop data collection, yet a single wrist view does not capture the global context required for base navigation. Adding a body-mounted camera entangles human walking with hand motion. Meanwhile, generative policies introduce hundreds of milliseconds of inference latency, during which the base advances past predicted waypoints, forcing backward corrections at action splices. This paper presents Mobile UMI, a hardware-free demonstration framework that addresses both gaps through three components. First, a dual-camera capture system records chest-centric global context and wrist-centric local interaction without any robot present. Second, a one-shot ChArUco-based spatial anchor unifies the chest and hand visual-inertial frames; the hand pose is then re-expressed relative to the chest to extract decoupled SE(3) manipulation and SE(2) base trajectories. Third, an asynchronous receding-horizon executor performs online state matching: each generated action chunk is realigned with the current physical pose so that expired waypoints are discarded before execution. The full system is evaluated on four long-horizon household tasks, achieving an average success rate of 83.8% over 100 trials per task. Controlled comparisons against ACT and Diffusion Policy show that the chest-relative label alone closes much of the gap; online state matching closes the remainder. These results indicate that, for mobile imitation learning under the tested conditions, explicit kinematic factorization combined with state-level latency alignment provides an effective solution without requiring architectural changes to the underlying policy class.

</details>


### [226] [SubTGraph: Large-Scale Subterranean Environment Synthesis with Controllable Topological Variability for Robotic Autonomy Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20917)
*F. Labra Caso,A. Saradagi,S. Fredriksson,S. Nordström,A. Koval,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SubTGraph框架，用于快速合成多层级地下环境，结合用户指定的拓扑、维度、纹理等约束，通过Dijkstra算法程序化生成多样化地下世界。开源了150个高变异性地下世界的代码库和数据库，并在结构语义分割、多智能体路径规划和LIO SLAM三个机器人案例中验证了其用于严苛评估自主栈不同层的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实地下环境难以进入，现有研究缺乏大规模仿真基准测试基础设施，导致验证结果仅局限于少数场景，统计评估不充分。

Method: SubTGraph框架：用户指定结构约束构建成本矩阵，引导经典Dijkstra算法，利用DARPA World Generator的拓扑度量瓦片程序化生成地下世界。

Result: 生成了150个高变异性的地下世界数据库；在三个案例中验证了框架的有效性：结构语义分割对标度量真值、多智能体路径规划识别算法模式与趋势、LIO SLAM在挑战性区域中识别故障案例。

Conclusion: SubTGraph填补了地下环境机器人自主栈大规模仿真基准测试的空白，为严苛统计评估提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Subterranean (SubT) environments have been a frontier for autonomous robotics, driven by the push for automation of mining operations and the interest in planetary exploration (Martian Lava Tubes). Due to the challenges involved in accessing real SubT environments, rigorous hardening of autonomy stacks in realistic simulation environments is critical. This article fills a well-known gap, which relates to the unavailability of a large-scale simulation-based benchmarking infrastructure for rigorous statistical evaluation of robotic autonomy, due to which it is common for SubT research articles to present validation results in a few environments at best.
  This article presents SubTGraph, a novel framework for rapid synthesis of multi-level SubT environments with high variability, incorporating user specifications related to topology, dimensionality, textures, etc., to generate distinct environments such as operational mines, natural caves and lava tubes. SubTGraph builds a cost matrix from user-specified structural constraints to guide the classical Dijkstra algorithm to procedurally generate SubT worlds utilizing topometric tiles from the DARPA World Generator. Three robotics case-studies are investigated to demonstrate the utility of SubTGraph for rigorous validation of different layers in the robotic autonomy stack. Structural semantic segmentation is validated against topometric ground truths, multi-agent path planning is widely tested for identification of patterns and trends in the algorithm behavior and LIO SLAM is stress-tested in challenging subterranean sections to identify failure cases. The SubTGraph world creation codebase is open-sourced (https://github.com/LTU-RAI/SubTGraph.git) along with a database consisting of 150 highly variable underground worlds.

</details>


### [227] [STEAM: A Training-Free Congestion-Aware Enhancement Framework for Decentralized Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20929)
*Mingyang Feng,Mengnuo Zhang,Shaoyuan Li,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STEAM是一种无需训练、仅测试时增强的框架，通过轻量级的拥塞感知引导，显著提升基于学习的去中心化多智能体路径规划（MAPF）算法的成功率、完工时间和解决方案成本，无需重新训练或修改模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的去中心化MAPF方法在复杂场景下容易因缺乏全局拥塞感知而导致路径冲突和效率低下，而集中式方法可扩展性差。作者希望在不改变原有模型结构、不重新训练、不依赖集中式规划器的前提下，提升去中心化策略的路径规划质量。

Method: STEAM在测试时注入轻量级的拥塞感知引导：(1) 空间可避免拥塞：通过更新智能体特定的cost-to-go信息来缓解；(2) 空间不可避免瓶颈：通过时间步上的logit修正来处理；(3) 涌现局部拥塞：基于邻居智能体修正后的cost-to-go图进行密度感知的logit修正。

Result: 在多个代表性学习型去中心化MAPF算法上的实验表明，STEAM持续提升了成功率、完工时间和解决方案成本，成功率提升高达60%，且仅带来极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: STEAM是一种高效、即插即用的测试时增强方法，能够在无需重新训练或修改架构的情况下显著提升去中心化MAPF算法的性能，具有很好的实用性和泛化性。

Abstract: We propose STEAM (Spatial, Temporal, and Emergent congestion Awareness for MAPF), a training-free test-time enhancement framework for learning-based decentralized Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) in discrete environments. Given a pretrained decentralized policy, STEAM requires no retraining, architectural modification, or replacement by a centralized planner. Instead, it injects lightweight congestion-aware guidance into the original policy execution. STEAM first rolls out the shortest paths induced by the current cost-to-go maps to identify potential future congestion hotspots. Spatially avoidable congestion is mitigated by updating agent-specific cost-to-go information, while spatially unavoidable bottlenecks are handled through temporal logit correction. In addition, emergent local congestion is reduced by a density-aware logit correction based on neighboring agents' corrected cost-to-go maps. Extensive experiments on representative learning-based decentralized MAPF algorithms show that STEAM consistently improves success rate, makespan, and solution cost, with success-rate gains of up to 60% and only minor computational overhead. The implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/STEAM-MAPF-7A62.

</details>


### [228] [WiXus: A Wheeled-Legged Robot with Wire-Driven Environmental Utilizing to Integrate Mobility and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20932)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并开发了WiXus机器人，将轮腿机构与线驱机构融合，通过利用外部环境支撑释放腿部运动自由度，使轮腿机器人的腿部不仅能用于 locomotion，还能在身体悬空时作为手臂执行物体操作（如救援、工具使用），拓展了轮腿机器人的操作域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮腿机器人仅作为 locomotion 专用平台，腿部无法脱离运动角色去执行物体操作或工具利用等任务，如何释放腿部潜力是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出一种融合轮腿机构与线驱机构的设计，利用线驱机构锚定外部环境来支撑身体，从而将腿部从 locomotion 角色中解放出来，使其可被重新用于操作任务。

Result: WiXus 实现了平面轮腿驱动 locomotion、通过线驱与轮腿协调实现攀爬悬崖等三维移动；在线驱悬吊身体状态下，腿部成功转化为手臂执行物体操作（救援玩具狗）和工具利用（用修枝剪采摘苹果模型）。

Conclusion: 利用线驱机构与环境互动的设计原则能有效扩展轮腿机器人的操作域，为未来多功能轮腿机器人提供新思路。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots, which have wheels at their feet and achieve high mobility by coordinating wheel drive and leg drive, have been developed. These robots have been developed purely as platforms specialized for locomotion. Therefore, they do not have a means to repurpose their legs for roles other than locomotion, such as object manipulation or tool utilization. In this paper, we address the problem of how to draw out the potential task-execution capability of the legs by freeing them from the roles of locomotion through external body support. To this end, we propose and develop a new robot, WiXus, which fuses a wheeled-legged mechanism with a wire-driven mechanism that utilizes the external environment. The developed WiXus demonstrates not only planar locomotion with wheeled-legged drive, but also three-dimensional mobility such as cliff climbing by coordinating wire-driven and wheeled-legged actuation. Furthermore, by suspending the body with wire-driven actuation, WiXus successfully repurpose its legs as arms to perform object manipulation, (e.g., rescuing a dog (stuffed animal)), and tool utilization (e.g., harvesting an apple (mockup) with loppers). This study demonstrates that the approach of utilizing the environment with wire-driven actuation is a new design principle that extends the operational domain of wheeled-legged robots.

</details>


### [229] [Component Influence-Driven Fastener Reduction for Robotic Disassemblability-Aware Design Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21026)
*Takuya Kiyokawa,Tomoki Ishikura,Shingo Hamada,Genichiro Matsuda,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个分析框架，通过CAD模型和CCC图，将机器人拆卸序列规划结果转化为零件影响分数，并以3D热图形式突出显示阻碍机器人操作的结构元件（主要是紧固件），指导设计者减少紧固件以优化产品可拆卸性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计者在产品设计阶段缺乏定量反馈来识别哪些结构元件会阻碍机器人拆卸操作，因此需要一种方法提供可操作的重设计指导。

Method: 利用CAD模型自动生成CCC图，将机器人拆卸序列规划结果转化为零件影响分数，投影为3D热图可视化结构障碍；通过分析模拟移除高影响力紧固件，评估结构约束、工具更换和机器人移动距离的减少，同时通过几何稳定性指标防止不安全修改。

Result: 在7种家用电器上实验：移除推荐紧固件后，结构约束减少8到132个，机器人移动距离缩短165到1675毫米，消除了不必要的工具更换操作。

Conclusion: 该框架能够成功定位冗余紧固件，通过减少结构依赖性和提高操作效率来优化产品设计，为设计者提供可操作的量化重设计建议。

Abstract: To accelerate automated remanufacturing, robotic disassembly must be considered during the product design phase. However, designers currently lack quantitative feedback to identify which structural elements hinder robotic operations. To address this, this study proposes an analytical framework that provides actionable redesign guidance focused on fastener reduction, as fasteners are numerous and ubiquitous components found in almost all manufactured products. Using a Computer-Aided Design (CAD) model and its automatically generated Contact-Connection-Constraint (CCC) graph, the framework translates robotic disassembly sequence planning outcomes into component influence scores. These scores reflect how often a component causes structural constraint violations or evaluation objective deteriorations in the robotic disassembly sequence. To visually highlight structural hindrances, the framework projects these scores onto the CAD geometry as 3D heatmaps. The system then analytically simulates the removal of highly influential fasteners. It reports the expected reductions in structural constraints, tool changes, and robot travel distances, while preventing structurally unsafe modifications by evaluating geometric stability metrics. Experiments on seven household appliances demonstrate that the framework successfully targets redundant fasteners. Removing the recommended fasteners simplified the structural dependencies by eliminating between 8 and 132 structural constraints on the graph depending on each product's structural configuration. Furthermore, it improved robotic operational efficiency by eliminating unnecessary tool change operations and shortening travel distances by 165 to 1675 millimeters wherever structurally permissible.

</details>


### [230] [Modeling and Control of a Pneumatic Morphing Soft Quadrotor based on the SOFA Framework for Dynamic Soft Robotic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21031)
*F. Labra Caso,V. Sumathy,P. Ferrentino,V. Vanderborght,J. Haluska,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SOFA的有限元方法，用于气动变形软体四旋翼飞行器的软体建模、动态仿真与控制。该方法保留了传统四旋翼动力学的物理可解释性和控制结构，同时捕捉了气动驱动软臂的复杂时变行为。仿真结果验证了所提建模框架和控制器设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四旋翼动力学模型无法准确描述气动软臂带来的复杂时变变形行为，需要一种既能保留传统控制结构又能捕捉软体动态特性的建模方法。

Method: 基于SOFA框架的有限元方法，将软体气动臂离散为四面体网格，采用弹性材料定律产生与真实动态行为匹配的内力；在内部空腔施加周期性和基于误差的控制信号；采用比例-积分(PI)控制器控制气动驱动以达到目标位置。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提出的建模框架和控制器设计在实现软臂变形控制方面是有效的。

Conclusion: 基于SOFA的有限元建模方法能够有效模拟气动变形软体四旋翼的动态行为，所提出的PI控制器能够实现对软臂变形和目标位置的有效控制。

Abstract: This article presents a novel SOFA based finite element method for the soft body modeling and the corresponding dynamic simulation and control of a pneumatic morphing soft quadrotor. The proposed modeling preserves the physical interpretability and control structure of traditional quadrotor dynamics, while capturing the complex, time-varying behavior of pneumatically actuated soft arms. In SOFA, the soft pneumatically actuated arms are discretized as a tetrahedral mesh following an elastic material law that produces internal forces adequate to the real dynamic behavior of the body. Pneumatic actuation governed by both periodic and error-based control signals is applied within the internal cavities to analyze the morphing capability. Finally, a proportional-integral controller is proposed to study the controlled dynamic behavior and morphing capabilities of the pneumatic arm, wherein the pneumatic actuation to the soft arm is controlled to achieve the desired target position. The simulation results show the effectiveness of the proposed novel modeling framework and the related controller design.

</details>


### [231] [Perception of Social Robots as Communication Partners in Healthcare for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21053)
*Hana Yamamoto,Carlotta Julia Mayer,Charlotte Raithel,Theresa Buchner,Christian Werner,Yasuhisa Hirata,Monika Eckstein,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究对35名70岁以上老年人进行对比实验，通过面部表情、心率变异性和主观问卷的多模态分析，发现社交机器人与人类作为交互伙伴在引发压力水平上无显著差异，且机器人交互时心率略低，表明老年人更能放松。社交机器人可作为有效交互伙伴，减轻照护者负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对全球照护人员短缺问题，探究社交辅助机器人能否成为老年人的有效交互伙伴，以及"积极提示"是否能增强交互效果，从而为缓解照护负担提供解决方案。

Method: 招募35名70岁以上老年参与者，进行人-机器人与人-人类交互的对比实验，采用多模态分析方法，整合面部表情数据、心率变异性(HRV)和主观问卷进行综合评估。

Result: 人机交互与人际交互在整体压力水平上无显著差异；面部表情分析显示机器人被接受为有效交互伙伴；生理数据显示机器人交互时心率略低，表明更放松的状态。

Conclusion: 社交机器人能在不引起心理压力的情况下与老年人互动，可通过执行结构化任务（如健康感知调查）减轻照护者负担；未来需解决机器人设计中"外观-内容不匹配"问题以促进更自然有效的交互。

Abstract: Addressing the global caregiver shortage through socially assistive robots necessitates a deep understanding of their psychological and physiological impacts on older adults during human-robot interaction (HRI). This study addresses whether social robots can serve as effective interaction partners compared to humans, and if "positive prompts" can similarly enhance these interactions. We conducted a comparative study with 35 participants (aged 70+). Our multi-modal analysis, integrating facial expression data, heart rate variability, and subjective questionnaires, revealed no significant differences in overall stress levels between human and robot interactions. Facial expression analysis confirmed that the robot was accepted as a valid interaction partner, while physiological data showed slightly lower heart rates during robot interactions, suggesting a more relaxed state compared to human-led sessions. These findings indicate that social robots can engage older adults without inducing psychological strain and are capable of alleviating caregiver burden by performing structured tasks, such as health-sensing surveys. Future work should address the identified "appearance-content mismatch" in robot design to facilitate even more natural and effective interactions.

</details>


### [232] [Anomaly-Informed Confidence Calibration for Vision-Based Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21109)
*Zhenjiang Mao,Jiawen Wu,Gabriel Wagner,Zhongzheng Zhang,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异常感知在线校准方法，通过融合世界模型中的感知异常分数（重建误差）和动态异常分数（认知不确定性与控制流统计），在不重新训练任何模型组件的情况下，利用测试时增强的轻量级温度缩放校准器，有效降低了自动驾驶漂移场景下的过度自信问题，在物理DonkeyCar平台上将平均期望校准误差从0.184降至0.116，相比最佳基线提升37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉异常检测方法（如自编码器重建误差）仅能捕捉视觉失真，但无法检测动态异常（如执行偏差、延迟），导致在测试时分布漂移下安全预测模型过度自信，存在感知-动力学鸿沟。

Method: 提出Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration方法：1) 从世界模型中提取互补的感知分数（重建误差）和动态分数（认知不确定性和控制流统计）；2) 融合两种异常分数；3) 使用轻量级温度缩放校准器结合测试时增强，选择性降低漂移下的过度自信。

Result: 在物理DonkeyCar上验证四种真实异常协议（黑暗、模糊、执行偏差、处理延迟），平均期望校准误差从0.184降至0.116，相对最佳基线提升37%。

Conclusion: 融合感知与动态异常信息的在线校准策略能有效弥合感知-动力学鸿沟，在不修改基础安全预测器且不重新训练的前提下显著提升分布漂移下的置信度校准质量。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimates are important for safely deploying vision-based controllers in autonomous racing, where safety predictions must be derived from camera images, yet modern predictors become dangerously overconfident under test-time distribution shifts. We identify a critical perception-dynamics gap in existing anomaly signals: widely used scores, such as autoencoder reconstruction error, capture visual corruptions but miss dynamics anomalies (e.g., actuation bias, latency), where images remain plausible while the trajectory degrades. To address this, we propose an Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration approach that, without retraining any model component, fuses two complementary anomaly scores extracted from a world model: a perceptual score from reconstruction error and a dynamics score from epistemic uncertainty and control-stream statistics. Based on these fused scores, a lightweight temperature-scaling calibrator leverages test-time augmentation to selectively reduce overconfidence under shift while preserving nominal-condition performance. Experiments on a physical DonkeyCar under four real-world anomaly protocols unseen during training (darkness, blur, actuation bias, processing latency) reduce average expected calibration error from 0.184 to 0.116, a 37% improvement over the best baseline, without modifying the base safety predictor.

</details>


### [233] [Benchmarking Empirical and Learning-Based Approaches for Feedforward Steering Control in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21111)
*Georg Jank,Mattia Piccinini,Sebastian Wenk,Phillip Pitschi,Johannes Betz,Boris Lohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种前馈转向控制器（两种基于学习、两种基于经验公式）在自动驾驶赛车中的表现，提出了基于多项式曲面拟合的新型EHD方法。结果表明，尽管学习型控制器在开环预测中误差更低，但在闭环测试中，所提出的EHD方法在鲁棒性和圈速上表现最佳，强调了在完整控制栈中评估前馈策略的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈转向控制是自动驾驶赛车分层控制架构的关键组成部分，旨在通过预测车辆逆横向动力学来减少反馈控制器的转向修正量，但目前缺乏对不同前馈控制方法的系统性比较与基准测试。

Method: 提出基于多项式曲面拟合的新EHD公式以捕捉速度相关的非线性转向行为；在基于阿布扎比自动驾驶赛车联盟真实赛道的高保真仿真框架中，使用双轨车辆动力学模拟器对两种学习型（基于学习）和两种经验型（基于解析公式）前馈控制器进行开环和闭环评估。

Result: 开环评估中学习型控制器预测误差最低；但闭环测试中，预测精度的提升并未转化为更优的路径跟踪性能或更快的圈速。所提出的EHD方法在闭环鲁棒性和圈速上总体表现最佳。

Conclusion: 前馈控制器的开环预测精度并不直接决定闭环性能，必须在完整的轨迹规划与控制软件栈中进行评估；基于多项式曲面拟合的EHD方法在闭环场景下更具鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Feedforward steering control is a key component of hierarchical control architectures for autonomous racing. The goal is to reduce steering corrections from the feedback controllers by predicting the vehicle's inverse lateral dynamics. This paper presents a systematic benchmark of two learning-based and two empirical (analytical) feedforward steering controllers. We introduce a new \acf{ehd} formulation based on a polynomial surface fit that captures velocity-dependent nonlinear steering behavior with minimal parametrization. We test the feedforward controllers in a high-fidelity simulation framework based on the real-world Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League competition, using a high-fidelity double-track vehicle dynamics simulator. Open-loop evaluation shows that the learning-based controllers achieve the lowest prediction errors; however, closed-loop testing reveals that this improved accuracy does not translate into superior path tracking performance or lap times, even after iterative fine-tuning. In contrast, the proposed EHD approach achieves the best overall closed-loop robustness and lap time, highlighting the necessity of evaluating feedforward strategies within the complete trajectory planning and control software stack. Our code is available at https://github.com/TUMRT/steering_ff_control.

</details>


### [234] [Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation via Active Spatial Brain and Generalizable Action Cerebellum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21133)
*Zhizhao Liang,Yi-Lin Wei,Xuhang Chen,Mu Lin,Yi-Xiang He,Zhexi Luo,Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体大模型的人形机器人全身操控框架，通过主动空间感知和通用化动作生成，在复杂3D环境中实现空间理解与动作泛化，无需任务特定真实机器人数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身操控任务面临两大挑战：1) 复杂3D环境中空间理解困难；2) 真实机器人数据有限且昂贵，限制了数据驱动模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出包含两个组件的框架：1) Active Spatial Brain（主动空间大脑）负责主动空间感知与决策，包括任务规划和子任务分解；2) Generalizable Action Cerebellum（通用化动作小脑）基于决策生成可执行机器人动作，无需任务特定真实数据。

Result: 在设计的空间操控任务集上，从空间感知理解和真实机器人任务表现两个维度进行评估，在多种任务和环境中均展现出强性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了人形机器人全身操控中的空间理解和动作泛化难题，验证了多智能体大模型在机器人操控中的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore spatial-aware humanoid whole-body manipulation task. Compared with tabletop settings, this task poses two key challenges: 1) Spatial understanding is challenging in complex 3D environments with diverse spatial relations. 2) Action generation is difficult to generalize, as limited and costly real-robot data restricts data-driven models generalization. To address these challenges, we propose a generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation framework that leverages the spatial perception and action generation capabilities of multi-agent large models. Specifically, our framework includes two components: Active Spatial Brain for active spatial perception and decision-making, and Generalizable Action Cerebellum for executable robot action generation. The first component actively perceives the spatial scene and makes decisions on task planning and subtask decomposition. The second component generate executable robot actions based on the decisions made by the first module without needs of task-specific real robot data. To benchmark our framework, we design a set of spatial manipulation tasks from two perspectives: evaluating spatial perception and understanding, and assessing real-robot task performance. The results demonstrate strong performance on both aspects across diverse tasks and environments.

</details>


### [235] [Safety-Critical Control for Smoothed Implicit Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21138)
*Haegu Lee,Yitaek Kim,Christoffer Sloth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对平滑隐式接触动力学的安全约束方法，通过边界聚焦滚动筛选平滑参数κ，并基于一阶泰勒近似的离散时间控制屏障函数框架，实现对接触力的鲁棒边界约束，消除标准CBF方法中的力违规问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑隐式接触动力学在接触丰富任务中实现了基于梯度的规划与控制，但安全保障面临挑战：平滑参数κ松弛了接触互补约束，影响接触力精度，且约束违规随κ变化呈非单调性，使得安全滤波器设计困难。

Method: 1) 提出边界聚焦滚动（boundary-focused rollouts）方法筛选平滑参数κ，通过比较安全裕度与近似误差来权衡；2) 基于接触力的一阶泰勒近似，构建离散时间控制屏障函数（CBF）框架；3) 引入固定鲁棒裕度补偿力的低估问题。

Result: 在四个接触丰富系统的仿真中，所提方法消除了标准CBF方法下观察到的力违规现象，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过有界接触力估计与鲁棒CBF框架的结合，能够在保持平滑隐式动力学优势的同时实现安全关键控制，为接触丰富任务的安全保障提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Smoothed implicit contact dynamics enables gradient-based planning and control for contact-rich tasks without predefined mode sequences. However, safety-critical control remains challenging because implicit contact dynamics makes safety-filter design nontrivial. The smoothing parameter $κ$ relaxes contact complementarity constraints, which makes the dynamics smooth but affects the contact force. This paper provides a method for bounding the actual contact force despite the use of relaxed complementarity constraints. We show that constraint violations can be non-monotonic in $κ$. Smaller $κ$ reduces force-approximation error, but it does not necessarily improve safety performance. To address this issue, we introduce boundary-focused rollouts to screen $κ$ by comparing the safety margin with the approximation error. We then develop a discrete-time control barrier function (CBF) framework based on a first-order Taylor approximation of the implicitly defined contact force. To account for possible force under-prediction, we augment the resulting safety constraint with a fixed robust margin. Simulations on four contact-rich systems show that the proposed method eliminates force violations observed under a standard CBF.

</details>


### [236] [EllipseLIO: Adaptive LiDAR Inertial Odometry with an Ellipsoid Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21150)
*Rowan Border,Margarita Chli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EllipseLIO，一种无需场景特定调参即可适应不同传感器和环境的实时LiDAR惯性里程计方法，在五个多样化数据集上平均里程计误差比第二名低38%，且是唯一在所有实验中均未发散的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIO方法在场景和传感器相似时表现良好，但难以在异构环境和传感器下保持鲁棒性，需针对不同场景手动调参。这限制了机器人在多样环境中的自主导航能力。

Method: 提出EllipseLIO，通过自适应LiDAR扫描滤波和配准方法，根据传感器能力和环境特征动态调整，无需特定场景参数调优，实现跨场景泛化。

Result: 在五个不同挑战性场景的数据集上与SOTA方法对比，EllipseLIO平均里程计误差比第二名低38%，且是唯一在所有实验中均未发散的方法。

Conclusion: EllipseLIO能够有效泛化到异构传感器和多样化环境，无需人工调参，为移动机器人在无GPS环境下的自主导航提供了更稳健的LIO解决方案。

Abstract: LiDAR Inertial Odometry (LIO) is a critical component for many mobile robots that need to navigate without relying on external positioning (e.g., GPS). Platforms that operate autonomously in different environments and with heterogeneous LiDAR sensors require a LIO approach that can adapt to these different scenarios without human intervention.
  Existing LIO approaches can typically provide reliable and accurate odometry in scenarios with similar environments and sensors when suitably tuned. However, many approaches struggle to retain robust odometry across heterogeneous environments and sensors while using a consistent configuration.
  This paper presents EllipseLIO, a real-time LIO approach that generalises between scenarios by using methods for LiDAR scan filtering and registration that adapt to the sensor capabilities and environment without requiring scenario-specific tuning. Experiments with EllipseLIO and state-of-the-art LIO approaches on five datasets with diverse and challenging scenarios demonstrate that EllipseLIO is the best-performing approach overall. It achieves a 38% lower odometry error on average than the second-best approach and is the only approach that does not diverge in any experiment. An open-source version of EllipseLIO will be available at github.com/v4rl-ucy/ellipselio.

</details>


### [237] [A Terrain-Adaptive epsilon-Constraint MPC for Uneven Terrain Kinodynamic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21188)
*Otobong Jerome,Geesara Kalathunga,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应epsilon约束方法集成到MPC框架中，通过地形描述符动态调整epsilon边界以实时探索帕累托前沿，并使用半参数模型（结合分析车辆动力学与稀疏高斯过程）捕获车辆-地形动力学。相比MPPI和GAKD基线，该方法实现了94%导航成功率，最大方向偏差降低24%，多目标权衡质量提升23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在崎岖地形上对类车机器人进行动力学规划需要同时优化路径效率和位姿稳定性等相互冲突的目标，现有方法难以实时探索帕累托前沿并处理车辆-地形复杂动力学。

Method: 提出自适应epsilon-constraint方法集成到MPC框架中，根据地形描述符动态调整epsilon边界；开发半参数模型，结合分析车辆动力学与基于相同地形描述符训练的稀疏高斯过程(SGP)来捕获车辆-地形动力学。

Result: 与MPPI和GAKD基线相比，导航成功率达到94%，最大方向偏差降低24%，多目标权衡质量提升23%。

Conclusion: 自适应epsilon-MPC方法能有效处理崎岖地形上类车机器人的多目标动力学规划问题，显著提升导航成功率和轨迹质量。

Abstract: Kinodynamic planning for car-like vehicles on uneven terrain requires simultaneously optimizing competing objectives such as path efficiency and pose stability. This work presents an adaptive epsilon-constraint method integrated into a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, where the epsilon bounds are dynamically adjusted based on terrain descriptors to explore the Pareto front in real time. To capture vehicle-terrain dynamics, we develop a semi-parametric model combining analytical vehicle dynamics with a Sparse Gaussian Process (SGP) trained on the same terrain descriptors. The proposed epsilon-MPC is evaluated against MPPI and GAKD baselines, achieving a 94% navigation success rate while reducing maximum orientation deviation by 24% and improving multi-objective trade-off quality by 23%.

</details>


### [238] [To Select or not to Select, that is the Question: Distilling Robot Skill Prediction into a Small Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21242)
*Haechan Mark Bong,Simon Roy,Euhid Aman,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人技能预测任务：将自然语言任务描述映射到机器人执行该任务所需的物理能力（如飞行、轮式、腿式等）。由于缺乏标注数据，作者利用LLM辅助生成和针对性标签审计构建了合成任务-技能数据集。训练一个约1.33亿参数的集成模型（两个微调句子编码器mpnet+MiniLM），在200任务数据集上达到83.5%的匹配准确率，显著优于Kimi K2（72.0%）、GPT-OSS-120B（71.5%）和Llama-4-Scout-17B（69.0%）等大模型。结果表明，针对固定机器人技能分类，基于合成数据训练的小型专用模型在任务路由性能上可超越通用大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人车队日益异构化（包括人形机器人、轮式机器人、四足机器人、无人机等），为任务选择合适机器人成为核心系统问题。现有方法缺乏将自然语言任务描述映射到机器人物理能力的标注数据。

Method: 采用LLM辅助生成和针对性标签审计构建合成任务-技能数据集；训练一个约1.33亿参数的集成模型，包含两个微调句子编码器（mpnet + MiniLM）。

Result: 在200任务数据集上，模型达到83.5%的任务-技能匹配准确率，显著优于Kimi K2（72.0%）、GPT-OSS-120B（71.5%）和Llama-4-Scout-17B（69.0%）等大模型。

Conclusion: 对于固定的机器人技能分类体系，基于合成数据训练的小型专用模型在车队级任务路由性能上可以超越参数量大得多的通用大语言模型。

Abstract: As robot fleets become more heterogeneous, including humanoids, rovers, quadrupeds, and drones, selecting the right robot for a task becomes a core systems problem. We study robot skill prediction: mapping a natural-language task description to the physical capabilities required to execute it, such as fly, wheels, legs, surface water, under water and hands. Since labelled data that maps natural-language task descriptions to robot's physical capabilities does not exist, we construct a synthetic task-to-skill dataset using LLM-assisted generation and targeted label auditing. Trained on this data, a ~133M-parameter ensemble of two fine-tuned sentence encoders (mpnet + MiniLM) reaches 83.5% task-to-skill matching on a stratified 200 task dataset, outperforming Kimi K2 (1T MoE) at 72.0%, GPT-OSS-120B at 71.5%, and Llama-4-Scout-17B at 69.0% under the same zero-shot prompt. These results suggest that, for fixed robot skill taxonomies, small specialized models trained on synthetic data can outperform much larger general-purpose LLMs for fleet-level task routing.

</details>


### [239] [Reinforcement Learning for Risk Adaptation via Differentiable CVaR Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21257)
*Xinyi Wang,Taekyung Kim,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端风险自适应框架，结合强化学习与基于条件风险价值屏障函数的微分二次规划安全层，用于在障碍物运动不确定性（高斯混合模型建模）下的人群导航，实现了名义控制输入、风险水平和安全边界的联合学习，在动态不确定拥挤环境中取得了安全、高效和泛化的最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在障碍物运动不确定的拥挤环境中规划路径十分困难，随机交互常导致过于保守的行为或效率降低，现有方法难以兼顾安全性与效率。

Method: 提出端到端风险自适应框架，将强化学习与基于条件风险价值(CVaR)屏障函数的可微二次规划安全层相结合，联合学习名义控制输入、风险水平和安全边界，施加显式概率安全约束。

Result: 在动态、不确定、拥挤环境中，跨不同障碍密度和机器人模型进行广泛评估，并在三种分布外泛化场景下测试。与基于优化、基于RL以及RL与优化集成的方法相比，所提方法在安全性、效率和不确定性下的泛化能力上均取得最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了上下文感知的风险自适应，在必要时才触发谨慎行为，从而在保证安全的同时提升效率，展现出最强的综合性能。

Abstract: Planning through crowded environments under uncertain obstacle motions remains difficult, as stochastic interactions often induce overly conservative behavior or reduced efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end risk adaptation framework for crowd navigation under obstacle-motion uncertainty modeled by a Gaussian mixture model. The framework combines reinforcement learning~(RL) with a differentiable quadratic-program safety layer based on Conditional Value-at-Risk~(CVaR) barrier functions, jointly learning nominal control input, risk level, and safety margin and enforcing explicit probabilistic safety constraints. This design enables context-aware adaptation, promoting efficient behavior while invoking caution only when necessary. We conduct extensive evaluations in dynamic, uncertain, and crowded environments across varying obstacle densities and robot models, and further assess generalization under three out-of-distribution cases. Comparisons across optimization-based, RL-based, and integrated RL and optimization methods are provided, and the proposed method is shown to deliver the strongest overall performance in safety, efficiency, and generalization under uncertainty.

</details>


### [240] [Learning Structural Latent Points for Efficient Visual Representations in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21258)
*Yicheng Jiang,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Zesen Gan,Junhao Li,Qiang Zhang,Jingkai Sun,Jiahang Cao,Mingyuan Sun,Xiangyu Yue,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合表征预训练框架——结构隐式点（Structural Latent Points），通过在点云自编码器的隐空间中插入逐点潜变量变分自编码器，联合正则化点特征和坐标服从高斯先验，从而融合隐式神经场的表达能力与显式几何基元的结构先验。同时设计了轻量级3DGS渲染管线，在RLBench、ManiSkill2和真实机器人平台上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D感知预训练方法要么采用完全隐式神经场（缺乏显式结构线索），要么采用完全显式几何基元（受限于分辨率且泛化弱），缺乏兼具表达能力与结构先验的表征。

Method: 1) 在点云自编码器的隐空间内插入逐点潜变量变分自编码器（point-wise latent VAE），联合正则化点特征和坐标；2) 学习紧凑隐变量，不编码精确几何但捕获粗糙形状与语义信息；3) 开发轻量级3DGS渲染管线，保持高效性并将表征能力留给前端隐模块。

Result: 在RLBench、ManiSkill2和真实机器人平台上，任务成功率、样本效率和视角/场景鲁棒性均持续优于强基线方法。消融实验验证了每个组件的关键性。

Conclusion: 提出的混合表征（结构隐式点）有效融合了隐式表达的灵活性与显式表达的结构信息，结合轻量渲染框架，为具身感知与操作任务提供了更优的预训练范式。

Abstract: Current 3D-aware pretraining methods for embodied perception and manipulation are largely built on differentiable rendering frameworks, producing either fully implicit neural fields or fully explicit geometric primitives. Implicit representations, while expressive, lack explicit structural cues, whereas explicit ones preserve geometry but suffer from resolution limits and weak generalization. To address these limitations, we propose a novel pretraining framework that learns a hybrid representation-structural latent points. Specifically, we insert a point-wise latent variational autoencoder into the latent space of a point-cloud autoencoder, jointly regularizing point-wise features and coordinates toward a Gaussian prior. The resulting compact latent preserves coarse structural tendencies, which do not encode precise geometry but capture richer rough shape and semantic information, effectively combining the expressiveness of implicit representations with the structural priors of explicit ones. In addition, informed by shared design choices in prior work, we develop a streamlined, efficient 3DGS-based rendering pipeline that is deliberately kept lightweight, improving efficiency while leaving greater representational capacity to the front-end latent module. Extensive evaluations on RLBench, ManiSkill2, and a real-robot platform demonstrate consistent gains in task success, sample efficiency, and robustness to viewpoint and scene variations over strong baselines. Ablation studies further confirm that each component of our framework is critical to overall performance.

</details>


### [241] [From swept contact to pose: Probe-aware registration via complementary-shape docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21398)
*Chen Chen,Yunwen Li,Yifan Xu,Xiangjie Yan,Chang Shu,Jianxia Hou,Shiji Song,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无标定的接触式配准方法，将问题建模为互补形状对接，通过3D FFT全局搜索和李代数局部优化实现亚毫米级精度，在牙科机器人上优于光学跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学配准方法存在标定链长、视线约束和制造误差等问题，需一种无标定、鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 将接触配注重构为物体与探针扫掠体积的互补形状对接；采用基于低差异SO(3)采样的3D FFT互相关进行全局到局部的搜索，随后通过李代数更新和分析接触灵敏度进行连续SE(3)精炼。

Result: 仿真中达到亚0.04 mm和亚0.4°精度；牙科机器上实现0.42 mm和3.75°精度，优于光学跟踪器。

Conclusion: 该方法无需外部传感器即可实现实用且精确的配准，适用于外科和工业机器人。

Abstract: Accurate registration between a prior model and the real scene is essential for high-precision robotic manipulation, yet optical methods suffer from long calibration chains, line-of-sight constraints, and fabrication errors. We propose a calibration-free alternative that reformulates contact registration as complementary-shape docking between the object and the probe's swept volume, explicitly accounting for probe geometry and leveraging both contact and non-contact evidence. Our solver integrates a global-to-local search via 3D FFT correlation over low-discrepancy SO(3) samples, then followed by continuous SE(3) refinement using Lie-algebra updates and analytic contact sensitivities. This pipeline yields efficient exploration and metric-grade convergence without fragile point correspondences. Simulation across free-form meshes achieved sub-0.04 mm and sub-0.4° accuracy and robustness to pose noise and contact loss. On a tooth-preparation robot, our method attained 0.42 mm and 3.75°, outperforming an optical tracker registration while requiring no external sensors. These results demonstrate a practical and precise registration strategy for surgical and industrial robots.

</details>


### [242] [MC-Risk: Multi-Component Risk Fields for Risk Identification and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21406)
*Maximilian Link,Yingjie Xu,Yingbai Hu,Yinlong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MC-Risk是一种基于鸟瞰图网格的多组件风险场，能够实现早期、校准且类别感知的风险定位。它线性组合了三个可解释模块：机动化智能体场（融合黑盒多模态轨迹预测与解析高斯环面）、VRU风险场（各向异性核替代各向同性行人斑块）和道路惩罚场（利用HD地图拓扑施加越野惩罚和车道感知风险暴露）。在RiskBench碰撞子集上的标准化定量评估中，MC-Risk取得了最佳总体风险定位和最早危险指示，并展示了作为MPC代价密度的即插即用规划接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险场方法缺乏标准化评估，且难以同时满足早期预警、校准概率和类别感知风险定位的需求。作者希望提出一种可解释、即插即用的多组件风险场，既能与黑盒轨迹预测器兼容，又能直接用于规划器。

Method: MC-Risk在BEV网格上线性组合三个模块：(1) 机动化智能体风险场：融合黑盒多模态轨迹预测与解析高斯环面，横向宽度随速度/曲率增长，高度随预测时域衰减；(2) VRU风险场：使用前向偏置的各向异性核，根据朝向和速度调整；(3) 道路惩罚场：利用HD地图拓扑，施加越野惩罚和同向/对向车道风险暴露。

Result: 在RiskBench碰撞子集上进行了首次标准化定量评估，MC-Risk取得最佳总体风险定位和最早危险指示。

Conclusion: MC-Risk能够作为MPC代价密度直接用于风险感知轨迹生成，无需额外训练，实现即插即用的规划接口。

Abstract: We present MC-Risk, a planner-aligned, multi-component risk field on a bird's-eye-view grid that yields early, calibrated, and class-aware risk localization. MC-Risk linearly composes three interpretable modules: (i) a motorized-agent field that fuses a black-box multimodal trajectory predictor with an analytic Gaussian-torus construction whose lateral width grows with speed/curvature and whose height attenuates with look-ahead; (ii) a VRU risk field that replaces isotropic pedestrian blobs with a forward-biased anisotropic kernel aligned to heading and speed; and (iii) a road penalty field that exploits full HD-map topology, imposing an off-road penalty and lane-aware risk exposure for same/opposite directions. We conduct, to our knowledge, the first standardized quantitative evaluation of a risk-field formulation on RiskBench's collision subset. MC-Risk attains the best overall risk localization and the earliest hazard indication. Finally, we demonstrate a plug-and-play planning interface by using the field as an MPC cost density, enabling risk-aware trajectory generation without additional training.

</details>


### [243] [PointACT: Vision-Language-Action Models with Multi-Scale Point-Action Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21414)
*Shizhe Chen,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointACT提出了一种双系统3D感知VLA策略，通过多尺度点-动作交互机制将层次化3D点云表示集成到动作解码中，在LIBERO和RLBench基准测试上相比现有方法提升了10%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖2D视觉表示，缺乏精细几何与空间定位推理能力，而这对3D环境中的精确鲁棒操作至关重要。

Method: PointACT采用双系统架构，通过多尺度点-动作交互机制和高效瓶颈窗口自注意力，使动作token能够密集关注局部几何细节和全局场景结构。

Result: 在RLBench-10Tasks套件上比最先进的预训练VLA提升10%的成功率，在视觉-语言骨干冻结、动作专家从头训练时增益更大。

Conclusion: 将层次化3D几何与预训练2D语义表示紧密耦合对鲁棒且空间定位准确的机器人控制至关重要，预训练3D表示在3D感知VLA策略中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation by leveraging large pretrained vision-language backbones. However, most existing VLAs rely primarily on 2D visual representations, which limit their ability to reason about fine-grained geometry and spatial grounding - capabilities that are essential for precise and robust manipulation in 3D environments. In this paper, we propose PointACT, a dual-system 3D-aware VLA policy that integrates hierarchical 3D point cloud representations directly into the action decoding process. PointACT employs a multi-scale point-action interaction mechanism with efficient bottleneck window self-attention, enabling evolving action tokens to densely attend to both local geometric detail and global scene structure. We evaluate PointACT on the LIBERO and RLBench benchmarks and systematically compare it against monolithic and dual-system VLA baselines, including variants augmented with point cloud inputs. PointACT achieves consistent improvements across both benchmarks, increasing success rates by 10% on the challenging RLBench-10Tasks suite over state-of-the-art pretrained VLAs, with even larger gains when the vision-language backbone is frozen and the action expert is trained from scratch. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that tightly coupling hierarchical 3D geometry with pretrained 2D semantic representations is critical for robust and spatially grounded robot control. Our results also highlight the promise of pretrained 3D representations for 3D-aware VLA policies.

</details>


### [244] [roto 2.0: The Robot Tactile Olympiad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21429)
*Elle Miller,Jayaram Reddy,Ayush Deshmukh,Trevor McInroe,David Abel,Oisin Mac Aodha,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robot Tactile Olympiad v2.0（roto 2.0），一个基于GPU并行化的触觉强化学习基准测试平台，覆盖四种机器人形态（16-24自由度），专注于仅依赖本体感知和触觉的盲操作任务，在Baoding球旋转任务上实现了比现有最优方法快一个数量级的性能提升（10秒内旋转13圈）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于触觉的强化学习研究碎片化严重，且过度集中在过饱和的朝向任务上，缺乏统一标准化的基准测试平台，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 提出roto 2.0基准平台：采用GPU并行化加速，覆盖四种不同机器人形态（16-DOF到24-DOF），专注于纯盲操作的端到端学习，仅使用本体感知和触觉传感信息，不依赖状态信息或知识蒸馏。同时开源环境并提供经过稳健调优的基线算法。

Result: 盲操作智能体在10秒内实现13次Baoding球旋转，比当前最优方法快一个数量级。

Conclusion: roto 2.0通过标准化触觉RL基准测试、开源环境和稳健基线，降低了研究门槛，使研究者能够聚焦于核心算法挑战而非繁琐的RL调参，推动了触觉强化学习领域的发展。

Abstract: Tactile-based reinforcement learning (RL) is currently hindered by fragmented research and a focus on over-saturated orientation tasks. We introduce v2 of the Robot Tactile Olympiad (\texttt{roto 2.0}), a GPU-parallelised benchmark designed to standardise tactile-based RL across four distinct robotic morphologies (16-DOF to 24-DOF). Unlike prior benchmarks, roto focuses on end-to-end "blind" manipulation, utilising only proprioception and tactile sensing without state information or distillation. We demonstrate a significant performance leap, with our blind agents achieving 13 Baoding ball rotations in 10 seconds, an order of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art speeds. By open-sourcing our environments and robustly tuned baselines, we reduce the barrier to entry and enable researchers to prioritise fundamental algorithmic challenges over tedious RL tuning. Website: https://elle-miller.github.io/roto/

</details>


### [245] [HITL-D: Human In The Loop Diffusion Assisted Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21460)
*Riley Zilka,Sergey Khlynovskiy,Allie Wang,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出人类在环扩散（HITL-D）共享控制框架，通过扩散策略与人类控制结合，在复杂操作任务中减少40%任务完成时间、降低37%感知负荷，并提升用户主观评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将人类专业知识与基于扩散的策略在共享控制中的整合，该方向目前研究相对不足。

Method: 提出HITL-D框架，结合扩散策略和人类控制，以场景点云和末端执行器笛卡尔位置为条件，提供自主末端执行器姿态更新，减少操纵杆控制轴数，降低脑力负荷。

Result: 在12名参与者的多任务用户研究中，平均任务完成时间减少40%，感知工作量降低37%，在独立性、直观性和信心方面的李克特评分均优于传统遥操作方法。

Conclusion: HITL-D有效整合了人类专业知识与自主辅助，在遥操作的客观和主观指标上均有显著改善。

Abstract: Autonomous manipulation systems have achieved remarkable capabilities, yet the integration of human expertise with diffusion-based policies in shared control remains relatively unexplored. In this paper, we propose Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion (HITL-D), a shared control framework that enhances user performance in multi-step, insertion, and fine manipulation tasks. HITL-D leverages a novel combination of diffusion-based policies and human control to provide autonomous end effector orientation updates conditioned on a scene point cloud and the Cartesian position of the end effector. This approach reduces the number of joystick control axes required, thereby lowering mental workload. In a multi-task user study with 12 participants, HITL-D reduced average task completion times by 40%, decreased perceived workload by 37%, and improved Likert-scale ratings for independence, intuitiveness, and confidence compared to traditional teleoperation methods. These results demonstrate that HITL-D effectively integrates human expertise with autonomous assistance, improving both objective and subjective aspects of teleoperation.

</details>
